										,@CrsrPrdAutoCalcPordPrjUsedType
										/*RZ14020529- End*/

	BEGIN

		/*-----------------------------------------------------*/

				/**/

			/**/

			IF(ISNULL(@InsertedPrjId,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/					
				SET @pordProjectId = @InsertedPrjId
			/**/

		/**/

		/**/

			IF(ISNULL(@ActAutoCalcNumRef,0) = 1)

				IF(ISNULL(@pordNumRef,'') <> '') 
				BEGIN
				/**/
					IF(ISNULL(@ActNotDupNumRef,0) = 1)
					BEGIN

							SET @pordNumRef = NULL

			/**/

				,@pordDeliveryDay
				,@pordDeliveryEndDay)
			SET @PreOrderId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()		
			IF(ISNULL(@PreOrderId,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				/*-------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*Multi Language*/
				IF(ISNULL(@pordDefaultSettingId,0) <> 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrder_Lang]
						([PreOrderID]
						,[LanguageID]
						,[pordTitle]
						,[pordSubject]
						,[pordSpecialWord]

					SELECT 
						 @PreOrderId
						,[LanguageID]
						,[Title]

					FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderDefaultSetting_Lang]

				END				
				/*-------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*---------------------- Item ---------------------------*/
				DECLARE @PrdUnitIRate FLOAt = NULL,@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto BIT = NULL
				,@TmpI NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TmpII NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TmpIII NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TmpPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
				,@ItemCount INT=NULL

				SELECT TOP(1) 
					 @pordIPrdColorId = [PrdColorId]
					,@pordIPrdUnitId = [PrdUnitId]
					/*,@pordIDescription = [PrdDescription]*/
					,@pordIPrdUnitIdI = [PrdUnitIdI]
					,@PrdUnitIRate = [PrdUnitIRate]
					,@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = [PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto]				
					,@pordIPrdNickname = [PrdNickname]
					,@PrdValidityDay = [PrdValidityDay]
					,@PrdValidityTime = [PrdValidityTime]    
				FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
				WHERE ProductID = @pordIPrdId

				IF((@PrdValidityDay IS NOT NULL))
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @IsCalcValidityExpireDate = 1

				BEGIN

					SET @IsCalcValidityExpireDate = 1
					SET @PrdValidityTimeHour = CAST((SUBSTRING(@PrdValidityTime,1,2)) AS INT)
					SET @PrdValidityTimeMinute = CAST((SUBSTRING(@PrdValidityTime,4,2)) AS INT)
				/**/
				END

				IF(ISNULL(@IsCalcValidityExpireDate,0) = 1)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					/*RZ4020720SET @pordIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(DAY, ISNULL(@PrdValidityDay,0), @pordDateTime)*/
					SET @pordIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(DAY, ISNULL(@PrdValidityDay,0), @FromDate)
					SET @pordIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(HOUR, ISNULL(@PrdValidityTimeHour,0), @pordIValidityExpireDate)
					SET @pordIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(MINUTE, ISNULL(@PrdValidityTimeMinute,0), @pordIValidityExpireDate)
					SET @pordIValidityExpireDatePersian = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](@pordIValidityExpireDate))
				/**/
				END						
				/*-------------------------------------------------------*/
				SET @ForamtAmount = 'N'
				IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
				BEGIN
					UPDATE @TblAmount
					SET [TotalAmount] = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](ISNULL([TotalAmount],0)))/*RZ14020626-Change Ceiling to Round*/
					WHERE [ID] <> @LastTblAmountId 
					SET @ForamtAmount = '0,000'
				END


				SET @pordIDescriptionI = (STUFF((SELECT CHAR(13)+CHAR(10) + /*N'��� ' +*/ ISNULL((SUBSTRING((SELECT [dbo].[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash]([OrdDate])),1,4)),'') +  N' = ' +  
													   (CASE WHEN ISNULL([TotalAmount],0) = 0
															THEN '0'
															ELSE (CAST(FORMAT(ISNULL([TotalAmount],0), @ForamtAmount)  AS NVARCHAR(300)))
													   END)	
													   + CHAR(13)+CHAR(10) + /*N'��� ������ = ' +*/ CHAR(13)+CHAR(10) + ISNULL([Formula],'') + CHAR(13)+CHAR(10) + N'----------------------------'
										FROM @TblAmount
										/*WHERE [ID] <> @LastTblAmountId*/ 		
										FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, '')) 			
										
				/*SET @pordIDescription = N'�� ����� ' + ISNULL(((SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithSlash](@pordDateTime))),'') + N' ��� ' + ISNULL(((SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithSlash](@pordIValidityExpireDate))),'')*/
				/*RZ14020529- Add fields and remove some parameters and change sp*/
				IF(ISNULL(@CrsrPrdAutoCalcPordItemDesc,'') <> '')
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @pordIDescription = @CrsrPrdAutoCalcPordItemDesc
					/*SET @pordIDescription = REPLACE(@pordIDescription,N'#FromDate#',ISNULL(((SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithSlash](@pordDateTime))),''))*/
					SET @pordIDescription = REPLACE(@pordIDescription,N'#FromDate#',ISNULL(((SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithSlash](@FromDate))),''))/*RZ14020720- Add field fromDate*/
					SET @pordIDescription = REPLACE(@pordIDescription,N'#ToDate#',ISNULL(((SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithSlash](@pordIValidityExpireDate))),''))
				/**/
				END
				/*RZ14020529- End*/
				/*-------------------------------------------------------*/
				SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@pordIQuantity,1) * ISNULL(@pordIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@pordIFCRI,1)	
				/*---------------- Calculate item ----------------*/
				SET @TmpII = ISNULL(@pordIPrice,0) - ISNULL(@pordIDiscount,0) - ISNULL(@pordIRound,0) + ISNULL(@pordIRoundPlus,0)
				SET @TmpIII = ((ISNULL(@pordIPrice,0) - ISNULL(@pordIRound,0) + ISNULL(@pordIRoundPlus,0)) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0)) - ISNULL(@pordIDiscountSum,0)
				/*---------------- Calculate tax -----------------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@pordITaxIndex,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@pordICalculateTax,0) = 1)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @pordITaxSum = ((ISNULL(@TmpIII,0)) * ISNULL(@pordITaxIndex,0) / 100)
					SET @pordITax = (ISNULL(@TmpII,0) * ISNULL(@pordITaxIndex,0) / 100)
				/**/
				END
				ELSE IF(ISNULL(@pordITax,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@pordICalculateTax,0) = 1 AND ISNULL(@TmpII,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @pordITaxSum = ISNULL(@pordITax,0) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0)
					SET @pordITaxIndex = (ISNULL(@pordITaxSum,0) * 100) / (ISNULL(@TmpII,0))
				/**/
				END
				/*---------------- Calculate item ----------------*/			
				SET @pordIPriceSum = @TmpIII
				SET @pordITotalPrice = ISNULL(@pordIPriceSum,0) + ISNULL(@pordITaxSum,0)
				/*--------- Calculate item by currency unit ------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@pordIPrice,0) = 0 )
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @pordIPrice = 0
					SET @pordIDiscountSum = 0

					SET @pordITaxSum = 0
					SET @pordITax = 0		
					SET @pordIPriceSum = 0
					SET @pordITotalPrice = 0
				/**/
				END
				/*------------------------------------------------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
				BEGIN
					/*RZ14020626-Change Ceiling to Round*/
					SET @pordIPrice = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordIPrice))
					SET @pordIDiscountSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordIDiscountSum))
					SET @pordIDiscount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordIDiscount))
					SET @pordITaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordITaxSum))
					SET @pordITax = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordITax))			
					SET @pordIPriceSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordIPriceSum))
					SET @pordITotalPrice = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordITotalPrice))
					/*RZ14020626-End*/
				END
				/*-------------------------------------------------------*/
				SET @ItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] POI WHERE POI.PreOrderId = @PreOrderId)
				SET @pordIOrderView = (ISNULL(@ItemCount,0) + 1) + (ISNULL(@ItemCount,0) * 4 ) - 1	
				/*-------------------------------------------------------*/
			
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems]
					([PreOrderId]
					,[pordIPrdId]
					,[pordIQuantity]
					,[pordIPrice]
					,[pordITotalPrice]

					,[UserId]
					,[FPId]
					,[Month1]
					,[RegisterDate]

					,[pordIDiscountPercent]
					,[pordIDiscountIPercent]
					,[pordIPrdUnitId]
					,[pordICurrencyUnitId]
					,[pordIOrderView]
					,[pordIFCR]
					,[pordITax]
					,[pordITaxIndex]
					,[pordICalculateTax]
					,[pordIFCRI]
					,[pordIWHId]
					,[pordIRound]
					,[AppType]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[FirstRegisterUserId]
					,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
					,[FirstRegisterDate]
					,[pordIRoundPlus]
					,[pordIPrdPriceGroupID]
					,[pordIPriceSum]
					,[pordIDiscountSum]
					,[pordIDiscountISum]
					,[pordITaxSum]								
					,[pordIPrdUnitIdI]
					,[pordIPrdUnitIRateResult]
					,[pordIPrdColorId]
					,[pordIPrdNickname]
					,[pordIValidityExpireDate]
					,[pordIValidityExpireDatePersian]
					,[pordIPrdAutoCalcRate]
					,[pordIPrdAutoCalcInflationRateInfo])/*RZ14020528- Change PrdAutoBill*/
				VALUES
					(@PreOrderId
					,@pordIPrdId
					,@pordIQuantity
					,@pordIPrice
					,@pordITotalPrice
					,@pordIDescription
					,@pordIDescriptionI

					,@pordIFCRI

					,@AppType
					,@DefaultLanguageId
					,@UserId
					,@RegisterDatePersian
					,@RegisterDate
					,@pordIRoundPlus
					,@pordIPrdPriceGroupID
					,@pordIPriceSum
					,@pordIDiscountSum
					,@pordIDiscountISum
					,@pordITaxSum																
					,@pordIPrdUnitIdI
					,@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult
					,@pordIPrdColorId
					,@pordIPrdNickname
					,@pordIValidityExpireDate
					,@pordIValidityExpireDatePersian
					,@pordIPrdAutoCalcRate
					,@pordIPrdAutoCalcInflationRateInfo)
				SET @PreOrderItemId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
				IF(ISNULL(@PreOrderItemId,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					/*Multi Language*/
					INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems_Lang]
						([PreOrderItemID]
						,[LanguageID]						
						,[pordIPrdNickname]
						,[pordIDescriptionI]/*RZ14020529- Add fields and remove some parameters and change sp*/						
						)
					SELECT
						@PreOrderItemId
						,[LanguageID]						
						,[PrdNickname]
						/*RZ14020529- Add fields and remove some parameters and change sp*/
						,(CASE WHEN ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) PLNI.PrdAutoCalcPordItemDesc 
											FROM [dbo].[WSProducts_Lang] PLNI 
											WHERE PLNI.ProductID = @CrsrProductID
											AND PLNI.[LanguageID] = PLN.[LanguageID]),'') <> ''
							   THEN (
									(REPLACE((
												(REPLACE((REPLACE((SELECT TOP(1) PLNI.PrdAutoCalcPordItemDesc 
																	FROM [dbo].[WSProducts_Lang] PLNI 
																	WHERE PLNI.ProductID = @CrsrProductID
																	/*AND PLNI.[LanguageID] = PLN.[LanguageID]),N'#FromDate#',ISNULL((FORMAT(@pordDateTime, 'yyyy/MM/dd', ISNULL((SELECT TOP (1) [LanguageCollation]   */
																	AND PLNI.[LanguageID] = PLN.[LanguageID]),N'#FromDate#',ISNULL((FORMAT(@FromDate, 'yyyy/MM/dd', ISNULL((SELECT TOP (1) [LanguageCollation]/*RZ14020720- Add field fromDate*/ 
																																	   FROM [dbo].[Languages] LL 
																																	   WHERE LL.[LanguageID] = PLN.[LanguageID]),'en-US'))),'')))
												,N'#ToDate#',ISNULL((FORMAT(@pordIValidityExpireDate, 'yyyy/MM/dd', 
																				ISNULL((SELECT TOP (1) [LanguageCollation]     
																				FROM [dbo].[Languages] LL 
																				WHERE LL.[LanguageID] = PLN.[LanguageID]),'en-US'))),'')))
									
									),'.','/'))
									)
							   ELSE NULL												
						 END)
						/*RZ14020529- End*/
					FROM [dbo].[WSProducts_Lang] PLN
					WHERE ProductID = @pordIPrdId
					AND [LanguageID] <> @DefaultLanguageId										
				/**/
				END
				/*-------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*----------------------  Calculate ---------------------*/
				EXEC [dbo].[WSspCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmounts] @PreOrderId, @Month1, @DefaultLanguageId, @DefaultLanguageId
				/*---------------------- Set PreOrders -------------------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@InsertedPords,'') <> '')
					SET @InsertedPords = ISNULL(@InsertedPords,'') + ','
				SEt @InsertedPords = ISNULL(@InsertedPords,'') + CAST(@PreOrderId AS NVARCHAR(200))
				/*-------------------------------------------------------*/
			/**/
			END			   		 
			/*---------------------------------------------------*/
		/**/
		END		
		/*---------------------------------------------------*/
		/*---------------- Issue PreOrder END ---------------*/
		/*---------------------------------------------------*/

		/*-----------------------------------------------------*/														    				 					
	/**/		
	FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrPrdAutoBill INTO @CrsrProductID,@CrsrPrdAutoCalcPordPrdId,@CrsrPrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemRate
										,@CrsrPrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemPrdGroups,@CrsrPrdAutoCalcDynamicRate,@CrsrPrdAutoCalcInflationLastDurationRate
										,@CrsrPrdValidity,@CrsrPrdValidityDay,@CrsrPrdValidityTime
										/*RZ14020529- Add fields and remove some parameters and change sp*/
										,@CrsrPrdAutoCalcPordVerifyAfterIssue,@CrsrPrdAutoCalcPordReferUser
										,@CrsrPrdAutoCalcPordExpireDynamicRate,@CrsrPrdAutoCalcPordExpireDay
										,@CrsrPrdAutoCalcPordItemDesc,@CrsrPrdAutoCalcPordTaxDetectFromLastOrd
										,@CrsrPrdAutoCalcPordTaxDefSettingId,@CrsrPrdAutoCalcPordNoTaxDefSettingId	

										,@CrsrPrdAutoCalcPordPrjTitleGroupId,@CrsrPrdAutoCalcPordPrjTitleId
										,@CrsrPrdAutoCalcPordPrjPostFix,@CrsrPrdAutoCalcPordPrjCnvsStatusGroupId
										,@CrsrPrdAutoCalcPordPrjFOCmpCanSee,@CrsrPrdAutoCalcPordPrjIsTicket
										,@CrsrPrdAutoCalcPordPrjUsedType
										/*RZ14020529- End*/
	END
	CLOSE CrsrPrdAutoBill
	DEALLOCATE CrsrPrdAutoBill
	/*---------------- Product Cursor End ---------------*/
	/*---------------------------------------------------*/
	/*---------------------------------------------------*/		
	COMMIT TRANSACTION AutoIssueMain
	/*---------------------------------------------------*/
	/*---------------------------------------------------*/	
/**/
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
/**/
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION AutoIssueMain	
	SET @ErrorMessage = ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') + ' ' + ISNULL(ERROR_MESSAGE(),'Error')
/**/
END CATCh
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END

						WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> 0)
	ORDER BY [pordDateTime],[pordNumber]

GO
-- [dbo].[WSspCalcAndUpdateBuyOrderAmounts]






/*RZ990902*/
/*RZ991008- Modify SumPriceAmount*/
/*RZ14000812- Modify condition in check status*/
/*RZ14010530- Add Check IsDecimal in CurrencyUnit*/
/*RZ14020324- Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
/*RZ14020626-Change Ceiling to Round*/
/*RZ14020807- Change status*/
/*RZ14020823- Add condition for update ordStatus*/
/*RZ14030301- Clac and update Amount in currencyI*/
/*RZ14030308- Add fields*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspCalcAndUpdateBuyOrderAmounts]
/* 	
	exec WSspCalcAndUpdateBuyOrderAmounts 292,3,1065,1065	
*/
(
@BuyOrderId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
/*RZ14010530- Add Check IsDecimal in CurrencyUnit*/
DECLARE @cuUnitIsDecimal BIT
SET @cuUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT TOP 1 [IsDecimal]
						FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] 
						WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = (SELECT bordCurrencyUnitId 
												  FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrder] 
												  WHERE BuyOrderID = @BuyOrderId))
/*RZ14010530- End*/
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*Calculate & Update the order*/	
/**/
DECLARE @SumPriceAmount NUMERIC(18,2)
/*RZ14020324- Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
DECLARE @SumQuantity FLOAT = NULL,@TotalDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TotalTaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
/*RZ14020324- End*/
SELECT  /*@SumPriceAmount = ISNULL(/*ROUND(*/
									CAST(SUM(
										(ISNULL(bordIQuantity, 0) * ISNULL( bordIFCR, 1)* ISNULL( bordIFCRI, 1)) 
										* 
										(ISNULL(bordICost, 0)- (ISNULL(bordIDiscount, 0) + ISNULL(bordIDiscountI, 0))) 
										+
										(ISNULL(bordITax, 0) * (ISNULL(bordIQuantity, 0) * ISNULL( bordIFCR, 1)* ISNULL( bordIFCRI, 1)))
									) AS NUMERIC(18,2))
								  /*, 0)*/,0)*/

		/*RZ991008- Modify SumPriceAmount*/
		@SumPriceAmount = ISNULL(CAST(SUM(ISNULL([bordICostSum],0)  + ISNULL([bordITaxSum],0)) AS NUMERIC(18,2)),0)
		/*RZ991008- End*/
		/*RZ14020324- Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
		,@SumQuantity = SUM(bordIQuantity)
		,@TotalDiscountSum = SUM(bordIDiscountSum)
		,@TotalTaxSum = SUM(bordITaxSum)
		/*RZ14020324- End*/
FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems]
WHERE BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderID
AND ISNULL(bordINotInSum, 0) = 0
/**/

IF(ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) >= 0)
BEGIN
	/**/
	DECLARE @bordItemCount INT, @bordItemsAmount NUMERIC(18,2),@bordDiscount NUMERIC(18,2),
	@bordSumAmount NUMERIC(18,2), @bordTax NUMERIC(18,2), @bordTaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2),
	@bordCalculateTax BIT,@bordTotalAmount NUMERIC(18,2),@bordTransportation NUMERIC(18,2),
	@bordDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2),@bordRound NUMERIC(18,2),@bordStatusId INT,
	@bordTotalAmountStr NVARCHAR(1000)
	/*RZ14030308- Add fields*/
	,@bordFacilityCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@bordFacilityCostPercent NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
	,@bordFreightCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@bordFreightCurrencyRate FLOAT = NULL,@bordFinalFreightCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
	,@bordCustomsFee NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@bordCustomsCurrencyRate FLOAT = NULL,@bordFinalCustomsFee NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
	,@bordCommission NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@bordCommissionCurrencyRate FLOAT = NULL,@bordFinalCommission NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
	,@bordTotalFinalCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
	/*RZ14030308- End*/
	/**/
	SELECT 
       @bordDiscount = [bordDiscount],
       @bordTaxIndex = [bordTaxIndex],
       @bordCalculateTax = [bordCalculateTax],
       @bordDiscountPercent = [bordDiscountPercent],
       @bordTransportation = [bordTransportation],
       @bordRound = [bordRound],
	   @bordStatusId= [bordStatusId] ,
	   /*RZ14030308- Add fields*/
	   @bordFacilityCost = [bordFacilityCost],
	   @bordFacilityCostPercent = [bordFacilityCostPercent],
	   @bordFreightCost = [bordFreightCost],
	   @bordFreightCurrencyRate = [bordFreightCurrencyRate],
	   @bordFinalFreightCost = [bordFinalFreightCost],
	   @bordCustomsFee = [bordCustomsFee],
	   @bordCustomsCurrencyRate = [bordCustomsCurrencyRate],
	   @bordFinalCustomsFee = [bordFinalCustomsFee],
	   @bordCommission = [bordCommission],
	   @bordCommissionCurrencyRate = [bordCommissionCurrencyRate],
	   @bordFinalCommission = [bordFinalCommission],
	   @bordTotalFinalCost = [bordTotalFinalCost]
	   /*RZ14030308- End*/
	FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrder] O
	WHERE O.[BuyOrderID] = @BuyOrderId
	/**/
	SET @bordItemsAmount = ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0)
	IF(@bordDiscountPercent IS NOT NULL AND @bordDiscountPercent > 0)
	BEGIN
		SET @bordDiscount = (ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) * ISNULL(@bordDiscountPercent,0)) / 100
	END
	/**/
	SET @bordSumAmount = ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) - ISNULL(@bordDiscount,0)
	/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@bordCalculateTax,0) = 1)
	BEGIN
		SET @bordTax = ISNULL(@bordSumAmount,0) * ISNULL(@bordTaxIndex,0) / 100
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @bordTax = 0
	END
	/**/
	SET @bordTotalAmount = ISNULL(ISNULL(@bordSumAmount,0) + ISNULL(@bordTax,0) + ISNULL(@bordTransportation,0) - ISNULL(@bordRound,0),0)
	/**/
	SET @bordItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] OI WHERE OI.BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId)
	/*---------------------------------------------------*/
	/*RZ14030308- Add fields*/	 
	IF(ISNULL(@bordFacilityCostPercent,0) <> 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @bordFacilityCost = (ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) * ISNULL(@bordFacilityCostPercent,0)) / 100
	/**/
	END
	/*------*/
	SET @bordFreightCost = ISNULL(@bordFreightCost,0)
	SET @bordFreightCurrencyRate = ISNULL(@bordFreightCurrencyRate,1)
	SET @bordFinalFreightCost = (ISNULL(@bordFreightCost,0) * ISNULL(@bordFreightCurrencyRate,1))
	/**/
	SET @bordCustomsFee = ISNULL(@bordCustomsFee,0)
	SET @bordCustomsCurrencyRate = ISNULL(@bordCustomsCurrencyRate,1) 
	SET @bordFinalCustomsFee = (ISNULL(@bordCustomsFee,0) * ISNULL(@bordCustomsCurrencyRate,1))
	/**/
	SET @bordCommission = ISNULL(@bordCommission,0)
	SET @bordCommissionCurrencyRate = ISNULL(@bordCommissionCurrencyRate,1)
	SET @bordFinalCommission = (ISNULL(@bordCommission,0) * ISNULL(@bordCommissionCurrencyRate,1))
	/**/
	SET @bordTotalFinalCost =  ISNULL(@bordTotalAmount,0) + ISNULL(@bordFacilityCost,0) + ISNULL(@bordFinalFreightCost,0) + ISNULL(@bordFinalCustomsFee,0) + ISNULL(@bordFinalCommission,0)
	/*RZ14030308- End*/
	/*---------------------------------------------------*/
	/*RZ14010530- Add Check IsDecimal in CurrencyUnit*/
	IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
	BEGIN	
		/*RZ14020626-Change Ceiling to Round*/
		SET @bordItemsAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordItemsAmount))					
		SET @bordSumAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordSumAmount))		
		SET @bordTax = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordTax))		
		SET @bordTotalAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordTotalAmount))		
		SET @bordDiscount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordDiscount))
		SET @bordTransportation = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordTransportation))
		SET @bordRound = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordRound))
		/*RZ14020324- Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/	
		SET @TotalDiscountSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0)))	
		SET @TotalTaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](ISNULL(@TotalTaxSum,0)))	
		/*RZ14020324- End*/
		/*RZ14020626-End*/
	END
	/*RZ14010530- End*/
	/*************** TotalAmountStr *******************/
	DECLARE @TotalAmoutI NVARCHAR(MAX),@TotalAmoutII NVARCHAR(MAX)
	IF(@bordTotalAmount IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN			
		SET @TotalAmoutI = SUBSTRING(CAST(@bordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), 1, CHARINDEX('.',CAST(@bordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))-1)                                    
		SET @TotalAmoutII = SUBSTRING(CAST(@bordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), CHARINDEX('.', CAST(@bordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))+1, 2)
		/**/
		IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1065)
		BEGIN
			SET @bordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @bordTotalAmountStr = @bordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			END
			SET @bordTotalAmountStr = @bordTotalAmountStr 
		END
		ELSE IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1025)
		BEGIN
			SET @bordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @bordTotalAmountStr = @bordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			END
			SET @bordTotalAmountStr = @bordTotalAmountStr
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			SET @bordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @bordTotalAmountStr = @bordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
			END
			SET @bordTotalAmountStr = REPLACE(@bordTotalAmountStr,'-',' ') 
		END
	END
	/**************************************************/
	/*Update*/
	UPDATE [dbo].[WSBuyOrder]
	SET  [bordItemCount] = ISNULL(@bordItemCount,0)
		,[bordItemsAmount] = ISNULL(@bordItemsAmount,0)
		,[bordSumAmount] = ISNULL(@bordSumAmount,0)
		,[bordTax] = ISNULL(@bordTax,0)
		,[bordTotalAmount] = @bordTotalAmount
		,[bordTotalAmountStr] = @bordTotalAmountStr
		,[bordDiscount] = ISNULL(@bordDiscount,0)
		,[bordTransportation] = ISNULL(@bordTransportation,0)
		,[bordRound] = ISNULL(@bordRound,0)
		,[Month1] = @Month1
		/*RZ14020324- Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
		,[bordItemsQty] = ISNULL(@SumQuantity,0)
		,[bordItemsDiscount] = ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0)
		,[bordItemsTax] = ISNULL(@TotalTaxSum,0)
		/*RZ14020324- End*/
		/*RZ14030308- Add fields*/
		,[bordFacilityCost] = @bordFacilityCost
		,[bordFacilityCostPercent] = @bordFacilityCostPercent
		,[bordFreightCost] = @bordFreightCost
		,[bordFreightCurrencyRate] = @bordFreightCurrencyRate 
		,[bordFinalFreightCost] = @bordFinalFreightCost 
		,[bordCustomsFee] = @bordCustomsFee
		,[bordCustomsCurrencyRate] = @bordCustomsCurrencyRate
		,[bordFinalCustomsFee] = @bordFinalCustomsFee
		,[bordCommission] = @bordCommission
		,[bordCommissionCurrencyRate] = @bordCommissionCurrencyRate 
		,[bordFinalCommission] = @bordFinalCommission
		,[bordTotalFinalCost] = @bordTotalFinalCost
		/*RZ14030308- End*/
	WHERE BuyOrderID = @BuyOrderId
	/**/
	IF(@bordTotalAmount IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @LangId INT
		/**/
		DECLARE Crsr CURSOR FOR
			SELECT LanguageID
			FROM [dbo].[Languages]		
			WHERE LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
			AND ISNULL(IsActive,0) = 1
		/**/
		OPEN Crsr
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
			While @@Fetch_Status = 0
			BEGIN	 
				DECLARE @ordTotalAmountStrLang NVARCHAR(1000)	
				/*-------------------------------------------*/				
				IF(@LangId = 1065)
				BEGIN
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					END
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang 
				END
				ELSE IF(@LangId = 1025)
				BEGIN
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					END
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
					END							
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = REPLACE(@ordTotalAmountStrLang,'-',' ')
				END
				/**/
				DECLARE @OrdIdLang INT
				SET @OrdIdLang = (SELECT [WSBuyOrderLangID] FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrder_Lang] WHERE BuyOrderID = @BuyOrderId AND LanguageID = @LangId)
				IF(@OrdIdLang IS NOT NULL)
				BEGIN
					UPDATE [dbo].[WSBuyOrder_Lang]
						SET [bordTotalAmountStr] = @ordTotalAmountStrLang
					WHERE [WSBuyOrderLangID] = @OrdIdLang						
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSBuyOrder_Lang]
						([BuyOrderID]
						,[LanguageID]
						,[bordTotalAmountStr])
					VALUES
						(@BuyOrderId
						,@LangId
						,@ordTotalAmountStrLang)
				END
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
			END
		CLOSE Crsr
		DEALLOCATE Crsr	
	END							
	/*==================================================*/
	/*================= Update status ==================*/	
	DECLARE @SumOrdPayAmount NUMERIC(18,2)
	SET @SumOrdPayAmount = ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL( SUM(bordpayAmount), 0) sumordpayAmount
									FROM dbo.WSBuyOrderPayments
									WHERE bordpayOrderId = @BuyOrderId
									AND ISNULL(bordpayDeActive,0) = 0/*RZ14020823- Add condition for update ordStatus*/),0)
	/*IF(@SumOrdPayAmount IS NOT NULL AND (@bordStatusId <> 4 AND @bordStatusId <> 5))*/
	IF(ISNULL(@SumOrdPayAmount,0) <> 0 AND (@bordStatusId <> 4 AND @bordStatusId <> 5))/*RZ14000812- Modify condition in check status*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		IF(@SumOrdPayAmount = 0 AND @bordStatusId <>1)
			SET @bordStatusId = 1
		/**/
		ELSE IF(@SumOrdPayAmount < @bordTotalAmount)/*RZ14020807- Change status - <>*/
			SET @bordStatusId = 2
		/**/
		ELSE IF(@SumOrdPayAmount >= @bordTotalAmount)/*RZ14020807- Change status - =*/
			SET @bordStatusId = 3
		/**/	 		
		/**/
		UPDATE [dbo].[WSBuyOrder]
		SET [bordStatusId] = @bordStatusId 
		WHERE [BuyOrderID] = @BuyOrderId
	/**/
	END
	/*==================================================*/
END	

/*==================================================*/	
/*RZ14030301- Clac and update Amount in currencyI*/
EXEC [dbo].[WSspCalcAndUpdateBuyOrderAmountsCurrency]	
@BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId,
@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID
/*RZ14030301- End*/
/*==================================================*/
/**/
END










GO
-- [dbo].[WSspCalcAndUpdateOrderAmounts]





/*RZ980921*/
/*RZ990902- Remove rial in ordTotalAmountStr*/
/*RZ991008- Modify SumPriceAmount*/
/*RZ14000812- Modify condition in check status*/
/*RZ14010530- Add Check IsDecimal in CurrencyUnit*/
/*RZ14020324- Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
/*RZ14020419- Add ordTspSettingId*/
/*RZ14020626-Change Ceiling to Round*/
/*RZ14020807- Change status*/
/*RZ14020823- Add condition for update ordStatus*/
/*RZ14021030- Re-Calculate items tax*/
/*RZ14030126- Add calculate OrderOtherAmounts*/
/*RZ14030301- Clac and update Amount in currencyI*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspCalcAndUpdateOrderAmounts]
/* 	
	exec WSspCalcAndUpdateOrderAmounts 42983,3,1065,1065	
*/
(
@OrderId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
/*----------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14021030- Re-Calculate items tax*/
DECLARE @CalcItemTaxResult INT = 0
EXEC [dbo].[WSspCalcAndUpdateOrderItemsTax]
@OrderId = @OrderId,
@Month1 = @Month1,
@Result = @CalcItemTaxResult OUTPUT	
/*RZ14021030- END*/
/*----------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14010530- Add Check IsDecimal in CurrencyUnit*/
DECLARE @cuUnitIsDecimal BIT
SET @cuUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT TOP 1 [IsDecimal]
						FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] 
						WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = (SELECT ordCurrencyUnitId 
												  FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] 
												  WHERE OrderID = @OrderId))
/*RZ14010530- End*/
/*RZ14020419- Add ordTspSettingId*/
DECLARE @ordTspSettingId INT 
SET @ordTspSettingId = (SELECT TOP(1) TspSettingId FROM [dbo].[TspSetting])
UPDATE  [dbo].[WSOrder]
SET  ordTspSettingId = @ordTspSettingId
WHERE [OrderID] = @OrderId
AND ISNULL(ordTspSettingId,0) = 0
/*RZ14020419- End*/
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*Calculate & Update the order*/	
/**/
DECLARE @SumPriceAmount NUMERIC(18,2)
/*RZ14020324- Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
DECLARE @SumQuantity FLOAT = NULL,@TotalDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TotalTaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
/*RZ14020324- End*/

SELECT  /*@SumPriceAmount = ISNULL(/*Round(*/
								CAST(SUM(
									(ISNULL(ordIQuantity, 0) * ISNULL( ordIFCR, 1)* ISNULL( ordIFCRI, 1)) *
									(ISNULL(ordIPrice, 0) - ( ISNULL(ordIDiscount, 0) + ISNULL(ordIDiscountI, 0) + ISNULL(ordIRound, 0)) 
									+ ISNULL(ordIRoundPlus, 0) )		
									+ ISNULL(ordITax, 0) * ISNULL(ordIQuantity, 0) * ISNULL( ordIFCR, 1)* ISNULL( ordIFCRI, 1)		
								) AS NUMERIC(18,2))
								/*, 0)*/ ,0)*/
		/*RZ991008- Modify SumPriceAmount*/
		@SumPriceAmount = ISNULL(CAST(SUM( ISNULL([ordIPriceSum],0) + ISNULL([ordITaxSum],0)) AS NUMERIC(18,2)),0)
		/*RZ991008- End*/
		/*RZ14020324- Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
		,@SumQuantity = SUM(ordIQuantity)
		,@TotalDiscountSum = SUM(ordIDiscountSum)
		,@TotalTaxSum = SUM(ordITaxSum)
		/*RZ14020324- End*/	
FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems]
WHERE OrderId = @OrderID
AND ISNULL(ordINotInSum, 0) = 0
IF(@SumPriceAmount IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	/**/
	DECLARE @ordItemCount INT, @ordItemsAmount NUMERIC(18,2),
	@ordInstallAmount NUMERIC(18,2), @ordDiscount NUMERIC(18,2),
	@ordSumAmount NUMERIC(18,2), @ordTax NUMERIC(18,2), @ordTaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2),
	@ordCalculateTax BIT,@ordTotalAmount NUMERIC(18,2),@ordTransportation NUMERIC(18,2),
	@ordDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2),@ordRound NUMERIC(18,2),@ordStatusId INT,
	@ordTotalAmountStr NVARCHAR(1000)
	/**/
	SELECT 
       @ordInstallAmount =[ordInstallAmount],
       @ordDiscount = [ordDiscount],
       @ordTaxIndex = [ordTaxIndex],
       @ordCalculateTax = [ordCalculateTax],
       @ordDiscountPercent = [ordDiscountPercent],
       @ordTransportation = [ordTransportation],
       @ordRound = [ordRound],
	   @ordStatusId= [ordStatusId] 
	FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] O
	WHERE O.[OrderID] = @OrderId
	/**/
	SET @ordItemsAmount = ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0)
	IF(@ordDiscountPercent IS NOT NULL AND @ordDiscountPercent > 0)
	BEGIN
		SET @ordDiscount = (ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) * ISNULL(@ordDiscountPercent,0)) / 100
	END
	/**/
	SET @ordSumAmount = ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) + ISNULL(@ordInstallAmount,0) - ISNULL(@ordDiscount,0)
	/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@ordCalculateTax,0) = 1)
	BEGIN
		SET @ordTax = ISNULL(@ordSumAmount,0) * ISNULL(@ordTaxIndex,0) / 100
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @ordTax = 0
	END
	/**/
	SET @ordTotalAmount = ISNULL(ISNULL(@ordSumAmount,0) + ISNULL(@ordTax,0) + ISNULL(@ordTransportation,0) - ISNULL(@ordRound,0),0)
	/**/
	SET @ordItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI WHERE OI.OrderId = @OrderId)
	/*---------------------------------------------------*/
	/*RZ14010530- Add Check IsDecimal in CurrencyUnit*/
	IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
	BEGIN	
		/*RZ14020626-Change Ceiling to Round*/
		SET @ordItemsAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordItemsAmount))					
		SET @ordSumAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordSumAmount))		
		SET @ordTax = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordTax))		
		SET @ordTotalAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordTotalAmount))		
		SET @ordDiscount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordDiscount))
		/*RZ14020324- Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/	
		SET @TotalDiscountSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0)))	
		SET @TotalTaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](ISNULL(@TotalTaxSum,0)))	
		/*RZ14020324- End*/
		/*RZ14020626-End*/
	END
	/*RZ14010530- End*/
	/*************** TotalAmountStr *******************/
	DECLARE @TotalAmoutI NVARCHAR(MAX),@TotalAmoutII NVARCHAR(MAX)
	IF(@ordTotalAmount IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN			
		SET @TotalAmoutI = SUBSTRING(CAST(@ordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), 1, CHARINDEX('.',CAST(@ordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))-1)                                    
		SET @TotalAmoutII = SUBSTRING(CAST(@ordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), CHARINDEX('.', CAST(@ordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))+1, 2)
		/**/
		IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1065)
		BEGIN
			SET @ordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @ordTotalAmountStr = @ordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			END
			SET @ordTotalAmountStr = @ordTotalAmountStr /*+ N' ����' *//*RZ990902- Remove rial in ordTotalAmountStr*/
		END
		ELSE IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1025)
		BEGIN
			SET @ordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @ordTotalAmountStr = @ordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			END
			SET @ordTotalAmountStr = @ordTotalAmountStr
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			SET @ordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @ordTotalAmountStr = @ordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
			END
			SET @ordTotalAmountStr = REPLACE(@ordTotalAmountStr,'-',' ') 
		END
	END
	/**************************************************/
	/*Update*/
	UPDATE [dbo].[WSOrder]
	SET  [ordItemCount] = ISNULL(@ordItemCount,0)
		,[ordItemsAmount] = ISNULL(@ordItemsAmount,0)
		,[ordSumAmount] = ISNULL(@ordSumAmount,0)
		,[ordTax] = ISNULL(@ordTax,0)
		,[ordTotalAmount] = @ordTotalAmount
		,[ordTotalAmountStr] = @ordTotalAmountStr
		,[ordDiscount] = ISNULL(@ordDiscount,0)
		,[Month1] = @Month1
		/*RZ14020324- Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
		,[ordItemsQty] = ISNULL(@SumQuantity,0)
		,[ordItemsDiscount] = ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0)
		,[ordItemsTax] = ISNULL(@TotalTaxSum,0)
		/*RZ14020324- End*/
	WHERE OrderID = @OrderId
	/**/
	IF(@ordTotalAmount IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @LangId INT
		/**/
		DECLARE Crsr CURSOR FOR
			SELECT LanguageID
			FROM [dbo].[Languages]		
			WHERE LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
			AND ISNULL(IsActive,0) = 1
		/**/
		OPEN Crsr
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
			While @@Fetch_Status = 0
			BEGIN	 
				DECLARE @ordTotalAmountStrLang NVARCHAR(1000)	
				/*-------------------------------------------*/				
				IF(@LangId = 1065)
				BEGIN
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					END
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang /*+ N' ����'*//*RZ990902- Remove rial in ordTotalAmountStr*/
				END
				ELSE IF(@LangId = 1025)
				BEGIN
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					END
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
					END							
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = REPLACE(@ordTotalAmountStrLang,'-',' ')
				END
				/**/
				DECLARE @OrdIdLang INT
				SET @OrdIdLang = (SELECT [WSOrderLangID] FROM [dbo].[WSOrder_Lang] WHERE OrderID = @OrderId AND LanguageID = @LangId)
				IF(@OrdIdLang IS NOT NULL)
				BEGIN
					UPDATE [dbo].[WSOrder_Lang]
						SET [ordTotalAmountStr] = @ordTotalAmountStrLang
					WHERE [WSOrderLangID] = @OrdIdLang						
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrder_Lang]
						([OrderID]
						,[LanguageID]
						,[ordTotalAmountStr])
					VALUES
						(@OrderId
						,@LangId
						,@ordTotalAmountStrLang)
				END
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
			END
		CLOSE Crsr
		DEALLOCATE Crsr	
	END							
	/*==================================================*/
	/*================= Update status ==================*/	
	DECLARE @SumOrdPayAmount NUMERIC(18,2)
	SET @SumOrdPayAmount = ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL( SUM(ordpayAmount), 0) sumordpayAmount
									FROM dbo.WSOrderPayments
									WHERE ordpayOrderId = @OrderId
									AND ISNULL(ordpayDeActive,0) = 0/*RZ14020823- Add condition for update ordStatus*/),0)
	/*IF(@SumOrdPayAmount IS NOT NULL AND (@ordStatusId <> 4 AND @ordStatusId <> 5))*/
	IF(ISNULL(@SumOrdPayAmount,0) <> 0 AND (@ordStatusId <> 4 AND @ordStatusId <> 5))/*RZ14000812- Modify condition in check status*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		IF(@SumOrdPayAmount = 0 AND @ordStatusId <>1)
			SET @ordStatusId = 1
		/**/
		ELSE IF(@SumOrdPayAmount < @ordTotalAmount)/*RZ14020807- Change status - <>*/
			SET @ordStatusId = 2
		/**/
		ELSE IF(@SumOrdPayAmount >= @ordTotalAmount)/*RZ14020807- Change status - =*/
			SET @ordStatusId = 3
		/**/	 		
		/**/
		UPDATE [dbo].[WSOrder]
		SET [ordStatusId] = @ordStatusId 
		WHERE [OrderID] = @OrderId
	/**/
	END
	/*==================================================*/
END	
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14030126- Add calculate OrderOtherAmounts*/
DECLARE @ResultCalcOrderOtherAmounts  INT = 0
EXEC [dbo].[WSspCalcAndUpdateOrderOtherAmounts]
@OrderId = @OrderId,
@Month1 = @Month1,
@CurrentDate = NULL,
@Result = @ResultCalcOrderOtherAmounts OUTPUT	
/*RZ14030126- End*/
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14030301- Clac and update Amount in currencyI*/
EXEC [dbo].[WSspCalcAndUpdateOrderAmountsCurrency]	
@OrderId = @OrderId,
@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID
/*RZ14030301- End*/
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END










GO
-- [dbo].[WSspCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmounts]




/*RZ990901*/
/*RZ991008- Modify SumPriceAmount*/
/*RZ14010530- Add Check IsDecimal in CurrencyUnit*/
/*RZ14020324- Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
/*RZ14020626-Change Ceiling to Round*/
/*RZ14021030- Re-Calculate items tax*/
/*RZ14030301- Clac and update Amount in currencyI*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmounts]
/* 	
	exec WSspCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmounts 4841,3,1033,1065	
*/
(
@PreOrderId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*----------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14021030- Re-Calculate items tax*/
DECLARE @CalcItemTaxResult INT = 0
EXEC [dbo].[WSspCalcAndUpdatePreOrderItemsTax]
@PreOrderId = @PreOrderId,
@Month1 = @Month1,
@Result = @CalcItemTaxResult OUTPUT	
/*RZ14021030- END*/
/*----------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14010530- Add Check IsDecimal in CurrencyUnit*/
DECLARE @cuUnitIsDecimal BIT
SET @cuUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT TOP 1 [IsDecimal]
						FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] 
						WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = (SELECT pordCurrencyUnitId 
												  FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] 
												  WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId))
/*RZ14010530- End*/
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*Calculate & Update the order*/	
/**/
/*RZ14020324- Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
DECLARE @SumQuantity FLOAT = NULL,@TotalDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TotalTaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
/*RZ14020324- End*/
DECLARE @SumPriceAmount NUMERIC(18,2)
SELECT 
	/*@SumPriceAmount = ISNULL(/*ROUND(*/
							CAST(SUM(
							 (ISNULL(pordIQuantity, 0) * ISNULL(pordIFCR, 1) * ISNULL(pordIFCRI, 1)) 
							 * 
							 (( ISNULL(pordIPrice, 0) - ( ISNULL(pordIDiscount, 0) + ISNULL(pordIDiscountI, 0) + ISNULL(pordIRound, 0))
							 /*ADBF960120*/
							 + ISNULL(pordIRoundPlus, 0)
							 ))	  
	 							+ (ISNULL( pordITax, 0) * ISNULL(pordIQuantity, 0) * ISNULL(pordIFCR, 1) * ISNULL(pordIFCRI, 1))
							) AS NUMERIC(18,2))
							/*, 0)*/,0)*/
	/*RZ991008- Modify SumPriceAmount*/
	@SumPriceAmount = ISNULL(CAST(SUM(ISNULL([pordIPriceSum],0) + ISNULL([pordITaxSum],0)) AS NUMERIC(18,2)),0)
	/*RZ991008- End*/	
	/*RZ14020324- Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
	,@SumQuantity = SUM(pordIQuantity)
	,@TotalDiscountSum = SUM(pordIDiscountSum)
	,@TotalTaxSum = SUM(pordITaxSum)
	/*RZ14020324- End*/	
FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems
WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderId
AND ISNULL(pordINotInSum, 0) = 0
/**/
IF(@SumPriceAmount IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	/**/
	DECLARE @pordItemCount  INT, @pordItemsAmount NUMERIC(18,2),
	@pordInstallAmount NUMERIC(18,2),@pordDiscount NUMERIC(18,2),
	@pordSumAmount NUMERIC(18,2),@pordTax NUMERIC(18,2),@pordTaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2),
	@pordCalculateTax BIT,@pordTotalAmount NUMERIC(18,2),@pordTransportation NUMERIC(18,2),
	@pordDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2),@pordRound NUMERIC(18,2),
	@pordTotalAmountStr NVARCHAR(1000)	
	/**/			
	SELECT 
       @pordInstallAmount =[pordInstallAmount],
       @pordDiscount = [pordDiscount],
       @pordTaxIndex = [pordTaxIndex],
       @pordCalculateTax = [pordCalculateTax],
       @pordDiscountPercent = [pordDiscountPercent],
       @pordTransportation = [pordTransportation],
       @pordRound = [pordRound]      
	FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] PO
	WHERE PO.[PreOrderID] = @PreOrderId
	/**/
	SET @pordItemsAmount = ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0)
	IF(@pordDiscountPercent IS NOT NULL AND @pordDiscountPercent > 0)
	BEGIN
		SET @pordDiscount = (ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) * ISNULL(@pordDiscountPercent,0)) / 100
	END
	/**/
	SET @pordSumAmount = ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) + ISNULL(@pordInstallAmount,0) - ISNULL(@pordDiscount,0)
	/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@pordCalculateTax,0) = 1)
	BEGIN
		SET @pordTax = ISNULL(@pordSumAmount,0) * ISNULL(@pordTaxIndex,0) / 100
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @pordTax = 0
	END
	/**/
	SET @pordTotalAmount = ISNULL(ISNULL(@pordSumAmount,0) + ISNULL(@pordTax,0) + ISNULL(@pordTransportation,0) - ISNULL(@pordRound,0),0)
	/**/
	SET @pordItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] POI WHERE POI.PreOrderId = @PreOrderId)
	/*---------------------------------------------------*/
	/*RZ14010530- Add Check IsDecimal in CurrencyUnit*/
	IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
	BEGIN	
		/*RZ14020626-Change Ceiling to Round*/
		SET @pordItemsAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordItemsAmount))					
		SET @pordSumAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordSumAmount))		
		SET @pordTax = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordTax))		
		SET @pordTotalAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordTotalAmount))		
		SET @pordDiscount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordDiscount))	
		
		/*RZ14020324- Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/	
		SET @TotalDiscountSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0)))	
		SET @TotalTaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](ISNULL(@TotalTaxSum,0)))	
		/*RZ14020324- End*/
		/*RZ14020626- End*/
	END
	/*RZ14010530- End*/
	
	/*************** ToTalAmountStr *******************/
	DECLARE @TotalAmoutI NVARCHAR(MAX),@TotalAmoutII NVARCHAR(MAX)
	IF(@pordTotalAmount IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN			
		SET @TotalAmoutI = SUBSTRING(CAST(@pordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), 1, CHARINDEX('.',CAST(@pordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))-1)                                    
		SET @TotalAmoutII = SUBSTRING(CAST(@pordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), CHARINDEX('.', CAST(@pordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))+1, 2)
		/**/
		IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1065)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @pordTotalAmountStr = @pordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			END
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = @pordTotalAmountStr /*+ N' ����'*/
		END
		ELSE IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1025)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @pordTotalAmountStr = @pordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			END
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = @pordTotalAmountStr
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @pordTotalAmountStr = @pordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
			END
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = REPLACE(@pordTotalAmountStr,'-',' ') 
		END
	END
	/**************************************************/
	/*Update*/
	UPDATE [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
	SET  [pordItemCount] = ISNULL(@pordItemCount,0)
		,[pordItemsAmount] = ISNULL(@pordItemsAmount,0)
		,[pordSumAmount] = ISNULL(@pordSumAmount,0)
		,[pordTax] = ISNULL(@pordTax,0)
		,[pordTotalAmount] = @pordTotalAmount
		,[pordTotalAmountStr] = @pordTotalAmountStr
		,[pordDiscount] = ISNULL(@pordDiscount,0)
		,[Month1] = @Month1 
		/*RZ14020324- Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
		,[pordItemsQty] = ISNULL(@SumQuantity,0)
		,[pordItemsDiscount] = ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0)
		,[pordItemsTax] = ISNULL(@TotalTaxSum,0)
		/*RZ14020324- End*/
	WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId
	/**/
	IF(@pordTotalAmount IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @LangId INT
		/**/
		DECLARE Crsr CURSOR FOR
			SELECT LanguageID
			FROM [dbo].[Languages]		
			WHERE LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
			AND ISNULL(IsActive,0) = 1
		/**/
		OPEN Crsr
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
			While @@Fetch_Status = 0
			BEGIN	 
				DECLARE @pordTotalAmountStrLang NVARCHAR(1000)	
				/*-------------------------------------------*/				
				IF(@LangId = 1065)
				BEGIN
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					END
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang /*+ N' ����'*/
				END
				ELSE IF(@LangId = 1025)
				BEGIN
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					END
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
					END							
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = REPLACE(@pordTotalAmountStrLang,'-',' ')
				END
				/**/
				DECLARE @PreOrdIdLang INT
				SET @PreOrdIdLang = (SELECT [WSPreOrderLangID] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder_Lang] WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId AND LanguageID = @LangId)
				IF(@PreOrdIdLang IS NOT NULL)
				BEGIN
					UPDATE [dbo].[WSPreOrder_Lang]
						SET [pordTotalAmountStr] = @pordTotalAmountStrLang
					WHERE [WSPreOrderLangID] = @PreOrdIdLang						
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrder_Lang]
						([PreOrderID]
						,[LanguageID]
						,[pordTotalAmountStr])
					VALUES
						(@PreOrderId
						,@LangId
						,@pordTotalAmountStrLang)
				END
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
			END
		CLOSE Crsr
		DEALLOCATE Crsr	
	END							
	
END		
/*==================================================*/	
/*RZ14030301- Clac and update Amount in currencyI*/
EXEC [dbo].[WSspCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmountsCurrency]	
@PreOrderId = @PreOrderId,
@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID
/*RZ14030301- End*/
/*==================================================*/
/**/
END
