-- [dbo].[WSspCalcAndUpdateStockSupplyFields]









/*RZ14020915*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspCalcAndUpdateStockSupplyFields]
/* 	
	exec WSspCalcAndUpdateStockSupplyFields 1,9,1065,1065	
*/
(
@StockSupplyId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @stsupItemsQty FLOAT,@stsupItemCount INT
/*----------------------------------------------*/
SELECT 
	@stsupItemsQty = (SUM(ISNULL([stsupIQuantity],0))) 				
FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems] 
WHERE [StockSupplyId] = @StockSupplyId
/*----------------------------------------------*/
SET @stsupItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems]  WHERE [StockSupplyId] = @StockSupplyId)
/*----------------------------------------------*/
UPDATE [dbo].[WSStockSupply]
SET	[stsupItemsQty] = @stsupItemsQty
	,[stsupItemCount] = @stsupItemCount
	,[Month1] = @Month1
WHERE [StockSupplyId] = @StockSupplyId
/*----------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END









GO
-- [dbo].[WSspCalcCompanyPrdIndexPrice]






/*NKR14020619*/
/*RZ14020706- Change text*/
/*RZ14020720- Add field fromDate*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[WSspCalcCompanyPrdIndexPrice]
(
	/*
		exec [WSspCalcCompanyPrdIndexPrice] @CompanyId=69145,@CalcDate='2023-08-22',@LanguageID=1065,@DefaultLanguageId=1065
	/*���� ��������� ���� �������� - �ǁ ǘӁ��      31612*/
	*/
	@CompanyId INT,
	@CalcDate DATETIME,
	@LanguageID INT,
	@DefaultLanguageId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
	/**/
	DECLARE @FromDate DATETIME = GETDATE()/*RZ14020720- Add field fromDate*/
	DECLARE @CalcRouteDesc NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,			
			@LanguageCollation NVARCHAR(MAX) = (SELECT L.LanguageCollation FROM Languages L WHERE L.LanguageID = @LanguageID)

	DECLARE @YearResource_TmpTable TABLE(
		[ID] INT IDENTITY(1, 1),
		[LanguageID] INT,
		[RS_Text] NVARCHAR(500)
	)
	INSERT INTO @YearResource_TmpTable(LanguageID, RS_Text)
	VALUES (1025, N'���'),
		   (1031, N'Jahr'),
		   (1033, N'Year'),
		   (1065, N'���')



	DECLARE @Amount_TmpTable TABLE(
		[ID] INT IDENTITY(1, 1),
		[TotalAmount] NUMERIC(18,2),
		[OrdDate] DATETIME,
		[Formula] NVARCHAR(MAX),
		[ErrorMessage] NVARCHAR(MAX)
	)

	DECLARE @OrderCmp_TmpTable TABLE(
		[ID] INT IDENTITY(1, 1),
		[OrderID] INT,
		[ordDateTime] DATETIME,
		[ordCompanyPersonId] INT,
		[ordProjectId] INT,

		[OrderItemID] INT,
		[ordIQuantity] FLOAT,
		[ordIPrice] NUMERIC(18, 2),
		[OrdIPrdId] INT,
		[OrdIValidityExpireDate] DATETIME,
		[ordIPrdAutoCalcRate] NUMERIC(18, 2),
		[ordINotInSum] BIT,

		[ProductID] INT,
		[PrdGroupID] INT,
		[PrdAutoCalcIndexed] BIT,
		[PrdAutoCalcDisabled] BIT,
		[PrdAutoCalcInflationLastDurationRate] NUMERIC(18, 2),
		[PrdAutoCalcIndexBreakDay] INT,
		[PrdAutoCalcPordPrdId] INT,
		[PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemRate] NUMERIC(18,2),
		[PrdAutoCalcDynamicRate] NVARCHAR(MAX),
		[PrdAutoCalcDynamicRateJustCompleteDuration] BIT,
		[PrdAutoCalcInflationLastYearNumChange] BIT,
		[PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemPrdGroups] NVARCHAR(MAX),
		[PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemInBreak] BIT,
		[PrdAutoCalcPriceFromIndex] BIT,
		[PrdAutoCalcPordExpireDynamicRate] BIT,
		[PrdAutoCalcInflationLastDuration] BIT,
		[PrdAutoCalcInflationLastYearNumChangeRate] NUMERIC(18, 2),
		[PrdAutoCalcPriceFromIndexRate] NUMERIC(18,2),
		[PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItem] BIT,
		[PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemAfterBreak] BIT,

		[IsCalculated] BIT
	)


	INSERT INTO @OrderCmp_TmpTable (
							[OrderID], 
							[ordDateTime], 
							[ordCompanyPersonId], 
							[ordProjectId], 

							[OrderItemID],
							[ordIQuantity],
							[ordIPrice],
							[OrdIPrdId],
							[OrdIValidityExpireDate],
							[ordIPrdAutoCalcRate],
							[ordINotInSum],

							[ProductID],
							[PrdGroupID],
							[PrdAutoCalcDisabled],
							[PrdAutoCalcIndexed],
							[PrdAutoCalcInflationLastDurationRate],
							[PrdAutoCalcIndexBreakDay], 
							[PrdAutoCalcPordPrdId],
							[PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemRate],
							[PrdAutoCalcDynamicRate],
							[PrdAutoCalcDynamicRateJustCompleteDuration],
							[PrdAutoCalcInflationLastYearNumChange],
							[PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemPrdGroups],
							[PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemInBreak],
							[PrdAutoCalcPriceFromIndex],
							[PrdAutoCalcPordExpireDynamicRate],
							[PrdAutoCalcInflationLastDuration],
							[PrdAutoCalcInflationLastYearNumChangeRate],
							[PrdAutoCalcPriceFromIndexRate],
							[PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItem],
							[PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemAfterBreak]
						)
	SELECT W.[OrderID], 
		   W.[ordDateTime], 
		   W.[ordCompanyPersonId], 
		   W.[ordProjectId], 

		   WI.[OrderItemID],
		   WI.[ordIQuantity],
		   WI.[ordIPrice],
		   WI.[ordIPrdId],
		   WI.[ordIValidityExpireDate],
		   WI.[ordIPrdAutoCalcRate],
		   WI.[ordINotInSum],


		   P.[ProductID],
		   P.[PrdGroupID],
		   P.[PrdAutoCalcDisabled],
		   P.[PrdAutoCalcIndexed],
		   P.[PrdAutoCalcInflationLastDurationRate],
		   P.[PrdAutoCalcIndexBreakDay], 
		   P.[PrdAutoCalcPordPrdId],
		   P.[PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemRate],
		   P.[PrdAutoCalcDynamicRate],
		   P.[PrdAutoCalcDynamicRateJustCompleteDuration],
		   P.[PrdAutoCalcInflationLastYearNumChange],
		   P.[PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemPrdGroups],
		   P.[PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemInBreak],
		   P.[PrdAutoCalcPriceFromIndex],
		   P.[PrdAutoCalcPordExpireDynamicRate],
		   P.[PrdAutoCalcInflationLastDuration],
		   P.[PrdAutoCalcInflationLastYearNumChangeRate],
		   P.[PrdAutoCalcPriceFromIndexRate],
		   P.[PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItem],
		   P.[PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemAfterBreak]
	  FROM WSOrder W 
	  LEFT JOIN WSOrderItems WI ON (WI.OrderId = W.OrderID)
	  LEFT JOIN WSProducts P ON (P.ProductID = WI.ordIPrdId
	  )
	 WHERE W.ordCompanyId = @CompanyId
	   AND W.OrderOprID IN(SELECT OPRI.OrderOprID FROM [dbo].[WSOrderOpr] OPRI WHERE OPRI.OprType = 1)
	   AND ISNULL(ordStatusId,0) <> 4
	 ORDER BY CAST(W.ordDateTime AS DATE)

	
	DECLARE @PrdIndexGroups_TmpTable TABLE(
		ID INT IDENTITY(1, 1),
		Tmp_Done BIT,
		
		[ProductID] INT,
		[PrdGroupID] INT,
		[PrdAutoCalcIndexed] BIT,
		
		[IndexOrderId] INT,
		[IndexOrdDate] DATETIME,
		[BeginFirstLoopDate] DATETIME,
		[EndLastLoopDate] DATETIME,

		[SumAllCmpOrdPrice] NUMERIC(18, 2),
		
		[PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemInBreak] BIT,
		[PrdAutoCalcPriceFromIndex] BIT,
		[BreakDay_PrdAutoCalcIndexBreakDay] INT,
		[SumOrdInBreakTime] NUMERIC(18, 2),
		[PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemPrdGroups] NVARCHAR(MAX),
		[RatePercent_PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemRate] NUMERIC(18, 2),
		[CalcRatePriceInBreakTime] NUMERIC(18, 2),
		
		[DyFormula_PrdAutoCalcDynamicRate] NVARCHAR(MAX),
		[DyLoopCnt] FLOAT,
		[DyMinLoopDayDu] INT,
		[DyMaxLoopDayDu] INT,
		[DyNeedLoopCompleteMorThanOne_PrdAutoCalcDynamicRateJustCompleteDuration] BIT,
		[SumOrdInAllDyLoop] NUMERIC(18, 2),
		[SumPriceInAllDyLoop] NUMERIC(18, 2),

		[LastYearRate_PrdAutoCalcInflationLastDurationRate] NUMERIC(18, 2),
		[LastYearNumChange_PrdAutoCalcInflationLastYearNumChange] BIT,

		[IssuePordPrdId_PrdAutoCalcPordPrdId] INT,
		
		[DetailCalcInfo] NVARCHAR(MAX),
		[DetailCalcInfoJs] NVARCHAR(MAX),

		[pordExpireDateTime] DATETIME,

		[CalculatedFinalAmount] NUMERIC(18, 2)
	)


	INSERT INTO @PrdIndexGroups_TmpTable(PrdGroupID)
	SELECT DISTINCT O.PrdGroupID FROM @OrderCmp_TmpTable O
	 WHERE ISNULL(O.PrdAutoCalcIndexed, 0) = 1



	/*Begin Loop_1_OF_2 From PrdIndexGroup*/
	begin /*Begin Loop_1_OF_2 From PrdIndexGroup*/

	WHILE ((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM @PrdIndexGroups_TmpTable G WHERE ISNULL(G.Tmp_Done, 0) = 0) > 0)
	BEGIN
		/**/
		DECLARE @ID INT = NULL,
				@Tmp_Done BIT = NULL,
				@ProductID INT = NULL,
				@PrdGroupID INT = NULL,
				@PrdAutoCalcIndexed BIT = NULL,
				@IndexOrderId INT = NULL,
				@IndexOrdDate DATETIME = NULL,
				@IndexrdPrice NUMERIC(18, 2) = 0,
				@BeginFirstLoopDate DATETIME = NULL,
				@EndLastLoopDate DATETIME = NULL,
				@SumAllCmpOrdPrice NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
				@PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemInBreak BIT = NULL,
				@PrdAutoCalcPriceFromIndex BIT = NULL,
				@BreakDay_PrdAutoCalcIndexBreakDay INT = NULL,
				@SumOrdInBreakTime NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
				@SumOrdInIndexOrdDate NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
				@PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemPrdGroups NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
				@RatePercent_PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemRate NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
				@CalcRatePriceInBreakTime NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
				@DyFormula_PrdAutoCalcDynamicRate NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
				@DyLoopCnt FLOAT = NULL,
				@DyMinLoopDayDu INT = NULL,
				@DyMaxLoopDayDu INT = NULL,
				@DyNeedLoopCompleteMorThanOne_PrdAutoCalcDynamicRateJustCompleteDuration BIT = NULL,
				@SumOrdInAllDyLoop NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
				@SumPriceInAllDyLoop NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
				@LastYearRate_PrdAutoCalcInflationLastDurationRate NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
				@LastYearNumChange_PrdAutoCalcInflationLastYearNumChange BIT = NULL,
				@IssuePordPrdId_PrdAutoCalcPordPrdId INT = NULL,
				@DetailCalcInfo NVARCHAR(MAX) = '',
				@DetailCalcInfoJs NVARCHAR(MAX) = '',
				@CalculatedFinalAmount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
				/**/
				@PrdAutoCalcInflationLastDuration BIT = NULL,
				@PrdAutoCalcInflationLastYearNumChangeRate NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
				@PrdAutoCalcPriceFromIndexRate NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
				@PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItem BIT = NULL,
				@PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemAfterBreak BIT = NULL,
				/**/
				@pordExpireDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
				@PrdAutoCalcPordExpireDynamicRate BIT
		/**/
		SELECT TOP 1 @ID = ID, @PrdGroupID = PrdGroupID FROM @PrdIndexGroups_TmpTable G WHERE ISNULL(G.Tmp_Done, 0) = 0
		/**/
		SELECT TOP 1 
			   @ProductID = OT.ProductID,
			   @PrdGroupID = OT.PrdGroupID,
			   @PrdAutoCalcIndexed = OT.PrdAutoCalcIndexed,
			   @IndexOrderId = OT.OrderID,
			   @IndexOrdDate = OT.ordDateTime,

			   /*@BeginFirstLoopDate = (DATEADD(DAY, (ISNULL(OT.PrdAutoCalcIndexBreakDay, 0) + 1), OT.ordDateTime)),*//*Begin Loop Date*/
			   @BeginFirstLoopDate = ISNULL(OT.[OrdIValidityExpireDate],(DATEADD(DAY, (ISNULL(OT.PrdAutoCalcIndexBreakDay, 0) + 1), OT.ordDateTime))),/*Begin Loop Date*/
			   @EndLastLoopDate = @CalcDate,

			   @IndexrdPrice = (ISNULL(OT.ordIPrice, 0) * ISNULL(OT.ordIQuantity, 1)),
			   
			   @BreakDay_PrdAutoCalcIndexBreakDay = OT.PrdAutoCalcIndexBreakDay,
			   @PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemPrdGroups = OT.PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemPrdGroups,
			   /*@RatePercent_PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemRate = OT.[PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemRate],*/
			   @RatePercent_PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemRate = ISNULL(OT.[ordIPrdAutoCalcRate],OT.[PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemRate]),
			   @IssuePordPrdId_PrdAutoCalcPordPrdId= OT.PrdAutoCalcPordPrdId,
			   @DyNeedLoopCompleteMorThanOne_PrdAutoCalcDynamicRateJustCompleteDuration = OT.PrdAutoCalcDynamicRateJustCompleteDuration,
			   @PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemInBreak = OT.PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemInBreak,
			   @PrdAutoCalcPriceFromIndex = OT.PrdAutoCalcPriceFromIndex,
			   
			   @PrdAutoCalcInflationLastDuration = OT.PrdAutoCalcInflationLastDuration,
			   @PrdAutoCalcInflationLastYearNumChangeRate = OT.PrdAutoCalcInflationLastYearNumChangeRate,
			   @PrdAutoCalcPriceFromIndexRate = OT.PrdAutoCalcPriceFromIndexRate,
			   @PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItem  = OT.PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItem,
			   @PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemAfterBreak  = OT.PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemAfterBreak,

			   @DyFormula_PrdAutoCalcDynamicRate = OT.PrdAutoCalcDynamicRate,
			   @LastYearRate_PrdAutoCalcInflationLastDurationRate = OT.PrdAutoCalcInflationLastDurationRate,
			   @LastYearNumChange_PrdAutoCalcInflationLastYearNumChange = OT.PrdAutoCalcInflationLastYearNumChange,
			   @PrdAutoCalcPordExpireDynamicRate = OT.PrdAutoCalcPordExpireDynamicRate
		  FROM @OrderCmp_TmpTable OT
		 WHERE ISNULL(OT.PrdAutoCalcIndexed, 0) = 1
		   AND OT.PrdGroupID = @PrdGroupID
		 ORDER BY OT.ordDateTime DESC, OT.OrderID DESC
		/**/
		/**/
		/**/
		DECLARE @AllLoopDayDu INT = ISNULL(DATEDIFF(DAY, @BeginFirstLoopDate, @CalcDate), 0),
				@CalcPriceFromIndex NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL
		/**/
		SET @FromDate = GETDATE()/*RZ14020720- Add field fromDate*/
		/*Check And Calc @PrdAutoCalcPriceFromIndex */
		IF (@AllLoopDayDu <= 0)
		BEGIN
			/**/
			IF (ISNULL(@PrdAutoCalcPriceFromIndex, 0) = 1)
			BEGIN
				/**/
				SET @CalcPriceFromIndex = @IndexrdPrice
				/**/
				IF (ISNULL(@PrdAutoCalcPriceFromIndexRate, 0) <> 0)
				BEGIN
					/**/
					SET @CalcPriceFromIndex = @IndexrdPrice + (ISNULL(@IndexrdPrice, 0) * ISNULL(@PrdAutoCalcPriceFromIndexRate, 0) / 100)

					IF (ISNULL(@CalcPriceFromIndex, 0) > 0)
						SET @DetailCalcInfo = ISNULL(@DetailCalcInfo, '') + 'Price From Index: ' + 
							CAST(ISNULL(@CalcPriceFromIndex, 0) AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' = ' + 
							CAST(ISNULL(@IndexrdPrice, 0) AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' + (' + 
							CAST(ISNULL(@IndexrdPrice, 0) AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' * ' + 
							CAST(ISNULL(@PrdAutoCalcPriceFromIndexRate, 0) AS NVARCHAR(50)) + '%)' + CHAR(13) + CHAR(10)

					/**/
				END
				/**/
			END
			/**/
		END
		ELSE IF (@AllLoopDayDu > 0)
		BEGIN
			/**/
			IF (ISNULL(@PrdAutoCalcPriceFromIndex, 0) = 1)
			BEGIN
				/**/
				SET @CalcPriceFromIndex = @IndexrdPrice

				SET @DetailCalcInfo = ISNULL(@DetailCalcInfo, '') + 'Price From Index: ' + 
							CAST(ISNULL(@CalcPriceFromIndex, 0) AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' = ' + CAST(ISNULL(@IndexrdPrice,0)  AS NVARCHAR(50)) + CHAR(13) + CHAR(10)
				/**/
			END
			/**/
		END
		/**/
		/**/
		/**/
		begin /*CalcRate*/
		/**/
			IF (ISNULL(@PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItem, 0) = 1)
			BEGIN
				/**/
				IF (ISNULL(@PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemInBreak, 0) = 1)
				BEGIN
					/**/
					SELECT @SumOrdInBreakTime = (ISNULL(SUM(ISNULL(OCT.ordIPrice, 0) * ISNULL(OCT.ordIQuantity, 1)), 0))
					  FROM @OrderCmp_TmpTable OCT
					 WHERE ISNULL(OCT.ordINotInSum, 0) = 0
					   AND ISNULL(OCT.PrdAutoCalcDisabled, 0) = 0
					   AND OCT.PrdGroupID IN (SELECT CAST(T.[value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemPrdGroups, ',') T WHERE ISNULL(T.[value], '') <> '')
					   AND ISNULL(OCT.PrdAutoCalcPriceFromIndex, 0) = 0
					   AND CAST(OCT.ordDateTime AS DATE) BETWEEN CAST(@IndexOrdDate AS DATE) AND CAST(@BeginFirstLoopDate AS DATE)
					/**/
					SET @CalcRatePriceInBreakTime = (@SumOrdInBreakTime * ISNULL(@RatePercent_PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemRate, 0)) / 100
					IF (ISNULL(@CalcRatePriceInBreakTime, 0) > 0)
						SET @DetailCalcInfo = ISNULL(@DetailCalcInfo, '') + 'Rate OrdItemPrice: ' + 
							CAST(ISNULL(@CalcRatePriceInBreakTime, 0) AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' = (' + 
							CAST(ISNULL(@SumOrdInBreakTime, 0) AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' * ' + 
							CAST(ISNULL(@RatePercent_PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemRate, 0) AS NVARCHAR(50)) + '%)' + CHAR(13) + CHAR(10)
					/**/
				END
				/**/
			END
			/**/
		end /*!CalcRate*/
		/**/
		/**/
		SET @DetailCalcInfo = ISNULL(@DetailCalcInfo, '') + 'Rate + OrdIndexPrice: ' + 
			CAST(ISNULL(@CalcRatePriceInBreakTime, 0) + ISNULL(@CalcPriceFromIndex, 0) AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' = ' + 
			CAST(ISNULL(@CalcRatePriceInBreakTime, 0) AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' + ' + 
			CAST(ISNULL(@CalcPriceFromIndex, 0) AS NVARCHAR(50)) + CHAR(13) + CHAR(10)

		SET @CalcRatePriceInBreakTime = ISNULL(@CalcRatePriceInBreakTime, 0) + ISNULL(@CalcPriceFromIndex, 0)
		/**/
		/**/
		/**/
		begin /*CalcDynamic*/
			/**/
			IF (@AllLoopDayDu > 0 AND ISNULL(@DyFormula_PrdAutoCalcDynamicRate, '') <> '' AND ISJSON(@DyFormula_PrdAutoCalcDynamicRate) = 1)
			BEGIN
				/**/
				DECLARE @DynamicRate_TmpTable TABLE(
						[FromDay] INT,
						[ToDay] INT,
						[Rate] NUMERIC(18,2)
					)
				DELETE @DynamicRate_TmpTable
				/**/
				INSERT INTO @DynamicRate_TmpTable ([FromDay], [ToDay], [Rate])
				SELECT [FromDay],
					   [ToDay],
					   [Rate]
				  FROM OPENJSON(@DyFormula_PrdAutoCalcDynamicRate)
				  WITH (
							[FromDay] INT '$.FromDay',
							[ToDay] INT '$.ToDay',
							[Rate] NUMERIC(18, 2) '$.Rate'
						)
				/**/
				Declare @CurrLoopFrom INT = 0 
				SELECT TOP 1 @DyMaxLoopDayDu = D.ToDay , @CurrLoopFrom = D.FromDay 
				  FROM @DynamicRate_TmpTable D 
				 WHERE (ISNULL(D.FromDay, 0) = 0 OR (ISNULL(D.FromDay, 0) <> 0) AND @AllLoopDayDu >= D.FromDay)
				   AND (ISNULL(D.ToDay, 0) = 0 OR (ISNULL(D.ToDay, 0) <> 0 AND @AllLoopDayDu <= D.ToDay))
				ORDER BY D.Rate DESC
				/**/
				IF (ISNULL(@DyMaxLoopDayDu, 0) <= 0) SET @DyMaxLoopDayDu = @CurrLoopFrom 
				/**/
				IF (ISNULL(@DyMaxLoopDayDu, 0) > 0)
				BEGIN
					/**/
					SET @DyLoopCnt = CAST(@AllLoopDayDu AS FLOAT) / CAST(@DyMaxLoopDayDu AS FLOAT)
					/**/
					IF (@DyLoopCnt > 1 AND ISNULL(@DyNeedLoopCompleteMorThanOne_PrdAutoCalcDynamicRateJustCompleteDuration, 0) = 1) 
						SET @DyLoopCnt = CAST(@DyLoopCnt AS INT)
					ELSE 
						SET @DyLoopCnt = CAST(@DyLoopCnt AS INT) + 1
					/**/
					DECLARE @DyLoopCntTmp FLOAT = @DyLoopCnt,
							@BeginLoopDate DATETIME = @BeginFirstLoopDate,
							@EndLoopDate DATETIME = NULL

					SET @SumPriceInAllDyLoop = @CalcRatePriceInBreakTime
					WHILE (@DyLoopCntTmp > 0)
					BEGIN
						/**/
						DECLARE @CurrLoopBegin INT = 0, @CurrLoopEnd INT = 0, @CurrLoopRate FLOAT = 0,
								@SumPriceInAllDyPerLoop NUMERIC(18, 2) = 0
						/**/
						SET @EndLoopDate = DATEADD(DAY, @DyMaxLoopDayDu, @BeginLoopDate)
						IF (@EndLoopDate > @CalcDate) SET @EndLoopDate = @CalcDate
						/**/
						SET @DetailCalcInfo = ISNULL(@DetailCalcInfo, '') + CHAR(13) + CHAR(10) + FORMAT(@EndLoopDate, 'yyyy', @LanguageCollation) + ' ' + (SELECT Y.RS_Text FROM @YearResource_TmpTable Y WHERE Y.LanguageID = @LanguageID) + ': ' + CHAR(13) + CHAR(10)
						/**/
						SELECT TOP 1 @CurrLoopBegin = D.FromDay, @CurrLoopEnd = D.ToDay, @CurrLoopRate = D.Rate
						  FROM @DynamicRate_TmpTable D 
						 WHERE (ISNULL(D.FromDay, 0) = 0 OR (ISNULL(D.FromDay, 0) <> 0) AND DATEDIFF(DAY, @BeginLoopDate, @EndLoopDate) >= D.FromDay)
						   AND (ISNULL(D.ToDay, 0) = 0 OR (ISNULL(D.ToDay, 0) <> 0 AND DATEDIFF(DAY, @BeginLoopDate, @EndLoopDate) <= D.ToDay))
						ORDER BY D.Rate DESC
						/**/
						IF (ISNULL(@CurrLoopEnd, 0) <= 0) SET @CurrLoopEnd = @CurrLoopBegin
						/**/
						IF (ISNULL(@CurrLoopRate, 0) > 0)
						BEGIN
							/**/
							DECLARE @CalcRatePriceInLoop NUMERIC(18, 2) = 0,
									@LoopSumOrdPrice NUMERIC(18, 2) = (
											SELECT (ISNULL(SUM(ISNULL(OCT.ordIPrice, 0) * ISNULL(OCT.ordIQuantity, 1)), 0))
											  FROM @OrderCmp_TmpTable OCT
											 WHERE ISNULL(OCT.ordINotInSum, 0) = 0
											   AND ISNULL(OCT.PrdAutoCalcDisabled, 0) = 0
											   AND OCT.PrdGroupID IN (SELECT CAST(T.[value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemPrdGroups, ',') T WHERE ISNULL(T.[value], '') <> '')
											   AND CAST(OCT.ordDateTime AS DATE) BETWEEN CAST(@BeginLoopDate AS DATE) AND CAST(@EndLoopDate AS DATE)
										)

							IF (ISNULL(@PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemAfterBreak, 0) = 1)
							BEGIN
								/**/
								SET @CalcRatePriceInLoop = (ISNULL(@LoopSumOrdPrice, 0) * ISNULL(@RatePercent_PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemRate, 0)) / 100
								IF (@LoopSumOrdPrice > 0)
									SET @DetailCalcInfo = ISNULL(@DetailCalcInfo, '') + 'Calc Rate Price In Loop: ' + 
										CAST(ISNULL(@CalcRatePriceInLoop, 0) AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' = (' + 
										CAST(ISNULL(@LoopSumOrdPrice, 0) AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' * ' + 
										CAST(ISNULL(@RatePercent_PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemRate, 0) AS NVARCHAR(50)) + '%)' + CHAR(13) + CHAR(10)
								/**/
							END
							

							SET @SumPriceInAllDyPerLoop = @CalcRatePriceInLoop + (((@SumPriceInAllDyLoop + @CalcRatePriceInLoop) * @CurrLoopRate) / 100)
							
							SET @DetailCalcInfo = ISNULL(@DetailCalcInfo, '') + 'Calc Price In Last Loop: ' + 
								CAST(ISNULL(@SumPriceInAllDyPerLoop, 0) AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' = ((' + 
								CAST(ISNULL(@SumPriceInAllDyLoop, 0) AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' + ' + CAST(ISNULL(@CalcRatePriceInLoop, 0)AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') * ' + 
								CAST(ISNULL(@CurrLoopRate, 0) AS NVARCHAR(50)) + '%)' + CHAR(13) + CHAR(10)

							/**/
							/**/
							/**/
							IF (ISNULL(@PrdAutoCalcPordExpireDynamicRate, 0) > 1)
							BEGIN
								/**/
								SET @pordExpireDateTime = DATEADD(DAY, @CurrLoopEnd, @BeginLoopDate)
								/**/
							END
							/**/
						END
						/**/
						SET @SumPriceInAllDyLoop = @SumPriceInAllDyLoop + @SumPriceInAllDyPerLoop
						SET @BeginLoopDate = DATEADD(DAY, 1, @EndLoopDate)
						SET @DyLoopCntTmp = @DyLoopCntTmp - 1
						/**/
					END
					/**/
				END
				/**/
				SET @FromDate = GETDATE()/*RZ14020720- Add field fromDate*/
				
			END
			ELSE 
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @FromDate = @BeginFirstLoopDate/*RZ14020720- Add field fromDate*/
			/**/
			END
			/**/
		end /*!CalcDynamic*/
		/**/
		/**/
		/**/
		begin /*Last Year*/
			/**/
			DECLARE @SumLastYearPrice NUMERIC(18, 2) = ISNULL(@SumPriceInAllDyLoop, @CalcRatePriceInBreakTime)
			SET @CalculatedFinalAmount = @SumLastYearPrice
			/**/
			IF (@DyLoopCnt >= 1) /* or (@AllLoopDayDu <= 0)*/
			BEGIN
				/**/
				IF (ISNULL(@PrdAutoCalcInflationLastDuration, 0) = 1 AND ISNULL(@LastYearRate_PrdAutoCalcInflationLastDurationRate, 0) > 0)
				BEGIN
					/**/
					SET @SumLastYearPrice = @SumLastYearPrice + ((@SumLastYearPrice * ISNULL(@LastYearRate_PrdAutoCalcInflationLastDurationRate, 0)) / 100)
					SET @CalculatedFinalAmount = @SumLastYearPrice

					SET @DetailCalcInfo = ISNULL(@DetailCalcInfo, '') + 'Calc Price In Loop Last Year: ' + /*RZ14020706- Change text*/
						CAST(ISNULL(@SumLastYearPrice, 0) AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' = ' +
						CAST(ISNULL(ISNULL(@SumPriceInAllDyLoop, @CalcRatePriceInBreakTime), 0) AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' + (' + 
						CAST(ISNULL(ISNULL(@SumPriceInAllDyLoop, @CalcRatePriceInBreakTime), 0) AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' * ' + 
						CAST(ISNULL(@LastYearRate_PrdAutoCalcInflationLastDurationRate, 0) AS NVARCHAR(50)) + '%)' + CHAR(13) + CHAR(10)
					/**/
				END
				/**/
			END
			ELSE IF (ISNULL(@LastYearNumChange_PrdAutoCalcInflationLastYearNumChange, 0) = 1 AND ISNULL(@PrdAutoCalcInflationLastYearNumChangeRate, 0) > 0)
			BEGIN
				/**/
				DECLARE @StartDateYear SMALLINT = CAST(FORMAT(@BeginFirstLoopDate, 'yyyy', @LanguageCollation) AS SMALLINT),
						@ToDateYear SMALLINT = CAST(FORMAT(@CalcDate, 'yyyy', @LanguageCollation) AS SMALLINT)
				/**/
				IF (@StartDateYear <> @ToDateYear)
				BEGIN
					/**/
					SET @SumLastYearPrice = @SumLastYearPrice + ((@SumLastYearPrice * ISNULL(@PrdAutoCalcInflationLastYearNumChangeRate, 0)) / 100)
					SET @CalculatedFinalAmount = @SumLastYearPrice

					SET @DetailCalcInfo = ISNULL(@DetailCalcInfo, '') + 'Calc Price In Change Last Year: ' + /*RZ14020706- Change text*/
						CAST(ISNULL(@SumLastYearPrice, 0) AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' = ' +
						CAST(ISNULL(ISNULL(@SumPriceInAllDyLoop, @CalcRatePriceInBreakTime), 0) AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' + (' + 
						CAST(ISNULL(ISNULL(@SumPriceInAllDyLoop, @CalcRatePriceInBreakTime), 0) AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' * ' + 
						CAST(ISNULL(@PrdAutoCalcInflationLastYearNumChangeRate, 0) AS NVARCHAR(50)) + '%)' + CHAR(13) + CHAR(10)
					/**/
				END
				/**/
			END
			/**/
		end /*!Last Year*/
		/**/
		/**/
		/**/
		/**/
		INSERT INTO @Amount_TmpTable(TotalAmount, OrdDate, Formula)
		VALUES(@CalculatedFinalAmount, ISNULL(@BeginLoopDate, @CalcDate), @DetailCalcInfo)
		SET @DetailCalcInfoJs = (SELECT * FROM @Amount_TmpTable ORDER BY ID DESC FOR JSON PATH)
		DELETE @Amount_TmpTable
		/**/
		UPDATE @PrdIndexGroups_TmpTable 
		   SET Tmp_Done = 1 ,
			   [ProductID] = @ProductID,
			   [PrdGroupID] = @PrdGroupID,
			   [PrdAutoCalcIndexed] = @PrdAutoCalcIndexed,
			   [IndexOrderId] = @IndexOrderId,
			   [IndexOrdDate] = @IndexOrdDate,
			   [BeginFirstLoopDate] = @BeginFirstLoopDate,
			   [EndLastLoopDate] = @EndLastLoopDate,
			   [SumAllCmpOrdPrice] = @SumAllCmpOrdPrice,
			   [PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemPrdGroups] = @PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemPrdGroups,
			   [RatePercent_PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemRate] = @RatePercent_PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemRate,
			   [CalcRatePriceInBreakTime] = @CalcRatePriceInBreakTime,
			   [PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemInBreak] = @PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemInBreak,
			   [PrdAutoCalcPriceFromIndex] = @PrdAutoCalcPriceFromIndex,
			   [BreakDay_PrdAutoCalcIndexBreakDay] = @BreakDay_PrdAutoCalcIndexBreakDay,
			   [SumOrdInBreakTime] = @SumOrdInBreakTime,
			   [DyFormula_PrdAutoCalcDynamicRate] = @DyFormula_PrdAutoCalcDynamicRate,
			   [DyLoopCnt] = @DyLoopCnt,
			   [DyMinLoopDayDu] = @DyMinLoopDayDu,
			   [DyMaxLoopDayDu] = @DyMaxLoopDayDu,
   			   [DyNeedLoopCompleteMorThanOne_PrdAutoCalcDynamicRateJustCompleteDuration] = @DyNeedLoopCompleteMorThanOne_PrdAutoCalcDynamicRateJustCompleteDuration,
			   [SumOrdInAllDyLoop] = @SumOrdInAllDyLoop,
			   [SumPriceInAllDyLoop] = @SumPriceInAllDyLoop,
			   [LastYearRate_PrdAutoCalcInflationLastDurationRate] = @LastYearRate_PrdAutoCalcInflationLastDurationRate,
			   [LastYearNumChange_PrdAutoCalcInflationLastYearNumChange] = @LastYearNumChange_PrdAutoCalcInflationLastYearNumChange,
			   [IssuePordPrdId_PrdAutoCalcPordPrdId] = @IssuePordPrdId_PrdAutoCalcPordPrdId,
			   [DetailCalcInfo] = @DetailCalcInfo,
			   [DetailCalcInfoJs] = @DetailCalcInfoJs,
			   [CalculatedFinalAmount] = @CalculatedFinalAmount,
			   [pordExpireDateTime] = @pordExpireDateTime
		 WHERE ID = @ID
		/**/
	END

	end/*!End Loop_1_OF_2 From PrdIndexGroup*/

	
	
	
	SELECT
		INDX.ProductID AS PrdId,
		(SELECT P.PrdName FROM WSProducts P WHERE P.ProductID = INDX.ProductID) AS PrdName,
		CalculatedFinalAmount AS CalculatedFinalAmount,
		INDX.DetailCalcInfoJs AS DetailCalcInfoJs,
		INDX.IndexOrderId AS IndexOrderId,
		'' AS ErrorMessage,
		(SELECT TOP 1 O.OrdIValidityExpireDate FROM @OrderCmp_TmpTable O WHERE O.OrderID = INDX.IndexOrderId AND O.ProductID = INDX.ProductID) AS ordIValidityExpireDate,
		[pordExpireDateTime] AS pordExpireDateTime,
		@FromDate AS [FromDate]/*RZ14020720- Add field fromDate*/
	FROM @PrdIndexGroups_TmpTable INDX

	/**/
END








GO
-- [dbo].[WSspCalculateCostSheet]





/*RZ990625*/
/*RZ14000531- Add ctshILength,ctshIWidth*/
/*RZ14020330- Add ctshMarkupSum*/
/*RZ14020802- Not Edit FpId*/
Create procedure [dbo].[WSspCalculateCostSheet]
(		
/*		
	exec WSspCalculateCostSheet 10,3,'2020-09-14','13990624',1,6,'dasti'		
*/
@CostSheetId INT,
@UserId	INT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8),
@FPId INT,
@Month1	SMALLINT,
@AppType VARCHAR(20)
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*==================== Define Variables ===================*/
DECLARE 
/*Costsheet*/
@ctshCurrencyRate FLOAT,
@ctshRoundRate FLOAT,
@ctshSumAmount NUMERIC(18,2),
@ctshTaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2),
@ctshTax NUMERIC(18,2),
@ctshTotalAmount NUMERIC(18,2), 
@ctshMarkup NUMERIC(18,2),
@ctshMarkupFormula NVARCHAR(MAX),
/*CostsheetValue*/
@CostSheetDyFieldValueId INT,
@ctshDyFieldFormula NVARCHAR(MAX),
@ctshDyFieldTitleFormula NVARCHAR(MAX),
/*SumFooter*/
@SumQuantity FLOAT,
@SumWeight FLOAT,
@SumTotalCost NUMERIC(18,2),
@SumDesctCurrencyTotalCost NUMERIC(18,2),
@SumTotalPrice NUMERIC(18,2),
/*RZ14000531- Add ctshILength,ctshIWidth*/
@SumLength FLOAT,
@SumWidth FLOAT,
/*RZ14000531- End*/
/**/
@Formula NVARCHAR(MAX),@Result NUMERIC(18,2), @StrCom NVARCHAR(MAX) = N'',
@ctshMarkupSum NUMERIC(18,2),@ctshMarkupSumFormula NVARCHAR(MAX)/*RZ14020330- Add ctshMarkupSum*/

/*==================== Define Table =======================*/
DECLARE @ItemTmpTable TABLE (	
	ColumnName NVARCHAR(500),
	ColumnValue NVARCHAR(500)	
)
/*==================== Set Variables ===================*/
SELECT TOP 1 
       @ctshCurrencyRate = [ctshCurrencyRate]
      ,@ctshRoundRate = [ctshRoundRate]
      ,@ctshSumAmount = [ctshSumAmount]
      ,@ctshTaxIndex = [ctshTaxIndex]
      ,@ctshTax = [ctshTax]
      ,@ctshTotalAmount = [ctshTotalAmount]
      ,@ctshMarkup = [ctshMarkup]
      ,@ctshMarkupFormula = [ctshMarkupFormula]  
	  /*RZ14020330- Add ctshMarkupSum*/
	  ,@ctshMarkupSum = [ctshMarkupSum]
	  ,@ctshMarkupSumFormula = [ctshMarkupSumFormula]
	  /*RZ14020330- End*/
FROM [dbo].[WSCostSheets]
WHERE [CostSheetId] = @CostSheetId

/**/
SELECT 	  
     @SumQuantity = SUM([ctshIQuantity])
	,@SumWeight = SUM([ctshIWeight]) 
	,@SumTotalCost = SUM([ctshITotalCost]) 
	,@SumDesctCurrencyTotalCost = SUM([ctshIDesctCurrencyTotalCost]) 	  
	,@SumTotalPrice = SUM([ctshITotalPrice])
	/*RZ14000531- Add ctshILength,ctshIWidth*/
	,@SumLength = SUM([ctshILength]) 
	,@SumWidth = SUM([ctshIWidth]) 
	/*RZ14000531- End*/ 	  
FROM [dbo].[WSCostSheetItems]
WHERE [CostSheetId] = @CostSheetId
/*==================== Fill TmpTables ==================*/
INSERT INTO @ItemTmpTable (ColumnName,ColumnValue)
	SELECT /*A*/
		'#A' + CAST(ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY CPRD.[OrderView],CPRD.[CostSheetProductId]) AS NVARCHAR(500))
		,CAST([ctshIWeight] AS NVARCHAR(500))				
	FROM [dbo].[WSCostSheetItems] CHI
	INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSCostSheetProducts] CPRD
	ON CHI.CostSheetProductId = CPRD.CostSheetProductId
	WHERE CHI.[CostSheetId] = @CostSheetId	
	/*RZ14000531- Add ctshILength,ctshIWidth*/
	UNION
	SELECT /*AA*/
		'#AA' + CAST(ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY CPRD.[OrderView],CPRD.[CostSheetProductId]) AS NVARCHAR(500))
		,CAST([ctshILength] AS NVARCHAR(500))				
	FROM [dbo].[WSCostSheetItems] CHI
	INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSCostSheetProducts] CPRD
	ON CHI.CostSheetProductId = CPRD.CostSheetProductId
	WHERE CHI.[CostSheetId] = @CostSheetId
	UNION
	SELECT /*AAA*/
		'#AAA' + CAST(ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY CPRD.[OrderView],CPRD.[CostSheetProductId]) AS NVARCHAR(500))
		,CAST([ctshIWidth] AS NVARCHAR(500))				
	FROM [dbo].[WSCostSheetItems] CHI
	INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSCostSheetProducts] CPRD
	ON CHI.CostSheetProductId = CPRD.CostSheetProductId
	WHERE CHI.[CostSheetId] = @CostSheetId
	/*RZ14000531- End*/
	UNION  /*B*/
	SELECT 
		'#B' + CAST(ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY CPRD.[OrderView],CPRD.[CostSheetProductId]) AS NVARCHAR(500))		
		,CAST([ctshIQuantity] AS NVARCHAR(500))		
	FROM [dbo].[WSCostSheetItems] CHI
	INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSCostSheetProducts] CPRD
	ON CHI.CostSheetProductId = CPRD.CostSheetProductId
	WHERE CHI.[CostSheetId] = @CostSheetId
	UNION /*C*/
	SELECT 
		'#C' + CAST(ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY CPRD.[OrderView],CPRD.[CostSheetProductId]) AS NVARCHAR(500))		
		,CAST([ctshICost] AS NVARCHAR(500))				
	FROM [dbo].[WSCostSheetItems] CHI
	INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSCostSheetProducts] CPRD
	ON CHI.CostSheetProductId = CPRD.CostSheetProductId
	WHERE CHI.[CostSheetId] = @CostSheetId
	UNION /*D*/
	SELECT 
		'#D' + CAST(ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY CPRD.[OrderView],CPRD.[CostSheetProductId]) AS NVARCHAR(500))		
		,CAST([ctshITotalCost] AS NVARCHAR(500))				
	FROM [dbo].[WSCostSheetItems] CHI
	INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSCostSheetProducts] CPRD
	ON CHI.CostSheetProductId = CPRD.CostSheetProductId
	WHERE CHI.[CostSheetId] = @CostSheetId
	UNION /*E*/
	SELECT 
		'#E' + CAST(ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY CPRD.[OrderView],CPRD.[CostSheetProductId]) AS NVARCHAR(500))		
		,CAST([ctshIDesctCurrencyCost] AS NVARCHAR(500))					
	FROM [dbo].[WSCostSheetItems] CHI
	INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSCostSheetProducts] CPRD
	ON CHI.CostSheetProductId = CPRD.CostSheetProductId
	WHERE CHI.[CostSheetId] = @CostSheetId
	UNION /*F*/
	SELECT 
		'#F' + CAST(ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY CPRD.[OrderView],CPRD.[CostSheetProductId]) AS NVARCHAR(500))		
		,CAST([ctshIDesctCurrencyTotalCost] AS NVARCHAR(500))			
	FROM [dbo].[WSCostSheetItems] CHI
	INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSCostSheetProducts] CPRD
	ON CHI.CostSheetProductId = CPRD.CostSheetProductId
	WHERE CHI.[CostSheetId] = @CostSheetId
	UNION /*H*/
	SELECT 
		'#H' + CAST(ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY CPRD.[OrderView],CPRD.[CostSheetProductId]) AS NVARCHAR(500))		
		,CAST([ctshIPrice] AS NVARCHAR(500))					
	FROM [dbo].[WSCostSheetItems] CHI
	INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSCostSheetProducts] CPRD
	ON CHI.CostSheetProductId = CPRD.CostSheetProductId
	WHERE CHI.[CostSheetId] = @CostSheetId
	UNION /*I*/
	SELECT 
		'#I' + CAST(ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY CPRD.[OrderView],CPRD.[CostSheetProductId]) AS NVARCHAR(500))		
		,CAST([ctshIPriceRounded] AS NVARCHAR(500))		
	FROM [dbo].[WSCostSheetItems] CHI
	INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSCostSheetProducts] CPRD
	ON CHI.CostSheetProductId = CPRD.CostSheetProductId
	WHERE CHI.[CostSheetId] = @CostSheetId
	UNION /*J*/
	SELECT 
		'#J' + CAST(ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY CPRD.[OrderView],CPRD.[CostSheetProductId]) AS NVARCHAR(500))				
		,CAST([ctshIPriceRounded] AS NVARCHAR(500))		
	FROM [dbo].[WSCostSheetItems] CHI
	INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSCostSheetProducts] CPRD
	ON CHI.CostSheetProductId = CPRD.CostSheetProductId
	WHERE CHI.[CostSheetId] = @CostSheetId
/*==================== Update DyFiledValues ============*/
DECLARE Crsr CURSOR  LOCAL FOR 
	SELECT
		 [CostSheetDyFieldValueId]
		,CHFV.[ctshDyFieldFormula]
		,CFT.[ctshDyFieldFormula] [ctshDyFieldTitleFormula]
	FROM [dbo].[WSCostSheetDyFieldValues] CHFV
	INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSCostSheetDyFieldTitle] CFT
	ON CHFV.[CostSheetDyFieldTitleId] = CFT.[CostSheetDyFieldTitleId]
	WHERE CHFV.[CostSheetId] = @CostSheetId
	AND CFT.[ctshDyFieldType] = 1 /*Formula*/
	ORDER BY CFT.[OrderView],CFT.[CostSheetDyFieldTitleId]	 		
/**/
OPEN Crsr
FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @CostSheetDyFieldValueId, @ctshDyFieldFormula,@ctshDyFieldTitleFormula
	WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
	BEGIN	 	
		/*-------------------------------------------*/				
		IF(ISNULL(@ctshDyFieldFormula,'') = '')
			SET @ctshDyFieldFormula = @ctshDyFieldTitleFormula
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@ctshDyFieldFormula,'') <> '')
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @Formula = @ctshDyFieldFormula
			/*Set DyField*/			
			SELECT @Formula = REPLACE(
								(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(UPPER(@Formula) AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))),
								(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(UPPER('[#'+ CFT.[ctshDyFieldTitleForFormula] + '#]') AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
								,CAST((CAST(CHFV.[ctshDyFieldValue] AS NUMERIC(18,2))) AS NVARCHAR(2000))) 
			FROM [dbo].[WSCostSheetDyFieldValues] CHFV
			INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSCostSheetDyFieldTitle] CFT
			ON CHFV.[CostSheetDyFieldTitleId] = CFT.[CostSheetDyFieldTitleId]
			WHERE CHFV.[CostSheetId] = @CostSheetId	
			AND CHFV.[ctshDyFieldValue] IS NOT NULL
			/*ORDER BY CFT.[OrderView],CFT.[CostSheetDyFieldTitleId]*/	 			
			/*Set Items*/
			SELECT @Formula = REPLACE(
								(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(UPPER(@Formula) AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))),
								(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(UPPER(ColumnName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
								,ColumnValue) 
			FROM @ItemTmpTable 
			WHERE ColumnValue IS NOT NULL			
			/*Other Fields*/

			/*RZ14000531- Add ctshILength,ctshIWidth*/			
			IF(@SumWidth IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET  @Formula = REPLACE(
								(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(UPPER(@Formula) AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))),
								'#KKK'
								,@SumWidth) 
			/**/
			END		
			/**/
			IF(@SumLength IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET  @Formula = REPLACE(
								(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(UPPER(@Formula) AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))),
								'#KK'
								,@SumLength) 
			/**/
			END
			/**/
			/*RZ14000531- End*/
			IF(@SumWeight IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET  @Formula = REPLACE(
								(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(UPPER(@Formula) AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))),
								'#K'
								,@SumWeight) 
			/**/
			END
			
			/**/
			IF(@SumQuantity IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET  @Formula = REPLACE(
								(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(UPPER(@Formula) AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))),
								'#L'
								,@SumQuantity) 
			/**/
			END
			/**/
			IF(@SumTotalCost IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET  @Formula = REPLACE(
								(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(UPPER(@Formula) AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))),
								'#M'
								,@SumTotalCost) 
			/**/
			END
			/**/
			IF(@SumDesctCurrencyTotalCost IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET  @Formula = REPLACE(
								(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(UPPER(@Formula) AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))),
								'#N'
								,@SumDesctCurrencyTotalCost) 
			/**/
			END
			/**/
			IF(@ctshMarkup IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET  @Formula = REPLACE(
								(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(UPPER(@Formula) AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))),
								'#O'
								,@ctshMarkup) 
			/**/
			END
			/**/
			/*RZ14020330- Add ctshMarkupSum*/			
			IF(@ctshMarkupSum IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET  @Formula = REPLACE(
								(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(UPPER(@Formula) AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))),
								'#U'
								,@ctshMarkupSum) 
			/**/
			END			
			/*RZ14020330- End*/
			/**/
			IF(@SumTotalPrice IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET  @Formula = REPLACE(
								(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(UPPER(@Formula) AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))),
								'#P'
								,@SumTotalPrice) 
			/**/
			END
			/**/
			IF(@ctshTaxIndex IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET  @Formula = REPLACE(
								(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(UPPER(@Formula) AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))),
								'#Q'
								,@ctshTaxIndex) 
			/**/
			END
			/**/
			IF(@ctshTax IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET  @Formula = REPLACE(
								(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(UPPER(@Formula) AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))),
								'#R'
								,@ctshTax) 
			/**/
			END
			/**/
			IF(@ctshSumAmount IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET  @Formula = REPLACE(
								(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(UPPER(@Formula) AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))),
								'#S'
								,@ctshSumAmount) 
			/**/
			END
			/**/
			IF(@ctshTotalAmount IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET  @Formula = REPLACE(
								(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(UPPER(@Formula) AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))),
								'#T'
								,@ctshTotalAmount) 
			/**/
			END
			/**/				
			/*==========================*/
			/*Calculate*/
			BEGIN TRY
			/**/
				SET @Result = NULL
				SET  @StrCom = N''
				SET @StrCom ='SELECT @Result = ' + @Formula
				EXECUTE sp_executesql @StrCom, N'@Result NUMERIC(18,2) OUTPUT',@Result = @Result OUTPUT
				/**/
				UPDATE [dbo].[WSCostSheetDyFieldValues]
				SET [ctshDyFieldValue] = @Result
					,[ctshDyFieldFormula] = @ctshDyFieldFormula
					,[UserId] = @UserId
					/*,[FPId] = @FPId*//*RZ14020802- Not Edit FpId*/
					,[Month1] = @Month1
					,[RegisterDate] = @RegisterDate
					,[RegisterDatePersian] = @RegisterDatePersian
					,[AppType] = @AppType					
				WHERE [CostSheetDyFieldValueId] = @CostSheetDyFieldValueId
			/**/
			END Try
			BEGIN CATCH /*When this is not run formula and there is an error*/
			/**/
				UPDATE [dbo].[WSCostSheetDyFieldValues]
				SET [ctshDyFieldValue] = NULL
					,[ctshDyFieldFormula] = @ctshDyFieldFormula
					,[UserId] = @UserId
					/*,[FPId] = @FPId*//*RZ14020802- Not Edit FpId*/
					,[Month1] = @Month1
					,[RegisterDate] = @RegisterDate
					,[RegisterDatePersian] = @RegisterDatePersian
					,[AppType] = @AppType					
				WHERE [CostSheetDyFieldValueId] = @CostSheetDyFieldValueId
			/**/
			END CATCH   
		/**/
		END
		/*-------------------------------------------*/
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @CostSheetDyFieldValueId, @ctshDyFieldFormula,@ctshDyFieldTitleFormula
	END
CLOSE Crsr
DEALLOCATE Crsr

			,[UserId] = @UserId

			,[Month1] = @Month1
			,[RegisterDate] = @RegisterDate
			,[RegisterDatePersian] = @RegisterDatePersian			  
			,[AppType] = @AppType			  
		WHERE [CostSheetId] = @CostSheetId
	/**/
	END Try
	BEGIN CATCH /*When this is not run formula and there is an error*/
	/**/
		UPDATE [dbo].[WSCostSheets]
		SET 
		    [ctshMarkupSum] = NULL			  
			,[UserId] = @UserId
			/*,[FPId] = @FPId*//*RZ14020802- Not Edit FpId*/
			,[Month1] = @Month1
			,[RegisterDate] = @RegisterDate
			,[RegisterDatePersian] = @RegisterDatePersian			  
			,[AppType] = @AppType			  
		WHERE [CostSheetId] = @CostSheetId
	/**/
	END CATCH   
/**/
END	
/*RZ14020330- End*/
/*==================== Update Markup ===================*/
SET @Formula = ''
DECLARE @ResultMarkUp FLOAT 
IF(ISNULL(@ctshMarkupFormula,'') <> '')
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @Formula = @ctshMarkupFormula
	/*Set DyField*/			
	SELECT @Formula = REPLACE(
						(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(UPPER(@Formula) AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))),
						(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(UPPER('[#'+ CFT.[ctshDyFieldTitleForFormula] + '#]') AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
						,CAST((CAST(CHFV.[ctshDyFieldValue] AS NUMERIC(18,2))) AS NVARCHAR(2000))) 
	FROM [dbo].[WSCostSheetDyFieldValues] CHFV
	INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSCostSheetDyFieldTitle] CFT
	ON CHFV.[CostSheetDyFieldTitleId] = CFT.[CostSheetDyFieldTitleId]
	WHERE CHFV.[CostSheetId] = @CostSheetId	
	AND CHFV.[ctshDyFieldValue] IS NOT NULL
	/*ORDER BY CFT.[OrderView],CFT.[CostSheetDyFieldTitleId]	*/ 
			
	/*Set Items*/
	SELECT @Formula = REPLACE(
						(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(UPPER(@Formula) AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))),
						(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(UPPER(ColumnName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
						,ColumnValue) 
	FROM @ItemTmpTable 
	WHERE ColumnValue IS NOT NULL

	/*Other Fields*/
	/*RZ14000531- Add ctshILength,ctshIWidth*/
	/**/
	IF(@SumWidth IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET  @Formula = REPLACE(
						(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(UPPER(@Formula) AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))),
						'#KKK'
						,@SumWidth) 
	/**/
	IF(@SumLength IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET  @Formula = REPLACE(
						(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(UPPER(@Formula) AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))),
						'#KK'
						,@SumLength) 
	/**/
	END
	
	END
	/**/

	/*RZ14000531- End*/
	IF(@SumWeight IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET  @Formula = REPLACE(
						(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(UPPER(@Formula) AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))),
						'#K'
						,@SumWeight) 
	/**/
	END
	/**/
	
	/**/
	IF(@SumQuantity IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET  @Formula = REPLACE(
						(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(UPPER(@Formula) AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))),
						'#L'
						,@SumQuantity) 
	/**/
	END
	/**/
	IF(@SumTotalCost IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET  @Formula = REPLACE(
						(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(UPPER(@Formula) AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))),
						'#M'
						,@SumTotalCost) 
	/**/
	END
	/**/
	IF(@SumDesctCurrencyTotalCost IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET  @Formula = REPLACE(
						(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(UPPER(@Formula) AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))),
						'#N'

	/**/
	END
	/**/
	IF(@ctshMarkup IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
	/**/

						'#O'
						,@ctshMarkup) 
	/**/
	END
	/**/
	/*RZ14020330- Add ctshMarkupSum*/	
	IF(@ctshMarkupSum IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET  @Formula = REPLACE(
						(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(UPPER(@Formula) AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))),
						'#U'
						,@ctshMarkupSum) 
	/**/
	END			
	/*RZ14020330- End*/
	/**/
	IF(@SumTotalPrice IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN

			,[RegisterDatePersian] = @RegisterDatePersian			  
			,[AppType] = @AppType			  
		WHERE [CostSheetId] = @CostSheetId
	/**/
	END CATCH   
/**/
END
/*==================== Update Items ====================*/

@CostSheetId  = @CostSheetId,		
@UserId = @UserId,
@FPId = @FPId,
@Month1 = @Month1,

@UserId = @UserId,

@AppType = @AppType
/*========================================================*/
/**/
END

/*RZ14030119*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspCheckBuyOrderHaveOrder]
/* 
	exec WSspCheckBuyOrderHaveOrder 370
*/		
(
@BuyOrderId INT 
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/	
	IF(ISNULL((
		ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL(bordIQuantity,0)) FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] WHERE BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId),0)
		-		
		ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL([ordIQuantity],0)) FROM(
					SELECT  
						ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL([ordIQuantity],0))      
								FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI
								CROSS APPLY ((SELECT [value] FROM STRING_SPLIT([BuyOrderItemIds], ',') L WHERE CAST(ISNULL([value],'0') AS INT) <> 0)) AS bordItems		
								WHERE ISNULL([BuyOrderItemIds],'') <>''
								AND CAST(ISNULL(bordItems.[value],'0') AS INT) = BOI.BuyOrderItemID
								AND OI.ordIPrdId = BOI.bordIPrdId
								),0) [ordIQuantity]				
					FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] BOI
					WHERE BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId
				) TMP						
		),0)	
		),0) = 0
	)
	BEGIN
		SELECT 1 AS Result
	END
	ELSE /* �ǘ��� ���ϡ �ǘ��� ����� �� ������ �� ����� ����� �ǘ��� ���� ���� �� �ǘ��� ���� ���� */
	/*���� ���� ���� �� ����� �ǘ��� ���� ���� �ǘ���� ���� ����*/
	BEGIN
		SELECT 0 AS Result
	END	
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[WSspCheckHaveCmpProjectByOrdIssuePrdId]








/*RZ14020601*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspCheckHaveCmpProjectByOrdIssuePrdId]
/* 
	exec WSspCheckHaveCmpProjectByOrdIssuePrdId  6156,10571,1033,1065
*/	
(
@CompanyId INT,
@ProductId INT ,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL 
)	
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*----------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @ProjectId INT = NULL,
@PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleGroupId INT = NULL,@PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleId INT = NULL,@PrdOrdIssuePrjPostFix NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
@PrdOrdIssuePrjUsedType INT = NULL,
/**/
@prjTitle NVARCHAR(100) = NULL, @prjPostFix NVARCHAR(200) = NULL, @prjTitleGroup NVARCHAR(200) = NULL
/*------------- Set variables ------------*/
SELECT TOP (1) 	   
	 @PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleGroupId = [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleGroupId]
	,@PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleId = [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleId]
	,@PrdOrdIssuePrjPostFix = [PrdOrdIssuePrjPostFix]
	,@PrdOrdIssuePrjUsedType = [PrdOrdIssuePrjUsedType]			
FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
WHERE [ProductID] = @ProductId
/*---------------------------*/
SET @prjTitle = (SELECT TOP(1) [ProjectTitle] FROM dbo.CrmProjectTitles PT WHERE [ProjectTitleID] = @PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleId)
SET @prjTitleGroup = (SELECT TOP(1) [ProjectTitleGroup] FROM dbo.[CrmProjectTitleGroups] PT WHERE [ProjectTitleGroupId] = @PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleGroupId)
SET @prjPostFix = @PrdOrdIssuePrjPostFix
/*----------------------------*/
SET @ProjectId = (SELECT TOP 1 P.ProjectID 
						FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] P 
						WHERE P.prjCompanyId = @CompanyId 
						AND ISNULL(prjIsFinished,0) = 0
						AND (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST((ISNULL((REPLACE((ISNULL(prjTitleGroup,'') + ISNULL(prjTitle,'') + ISNULL(prjPostFix,'')),' ' ,'')),'')) AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
						= 
						(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST((ISNULL((REPLACE((ISNULL(@prjTitleGroup,'') + ISNULL(@prjTitle,'') + ISNULL(@prjPostFix,'')),' ' ,'')),'')) AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
					)
IF(ISNULL(@PrdOrdIssuePrjUsedType,0) = 1)/*(Issue in an existing project)*/
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@ProjectId,0) = 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @ProjectId = -1
	/**/
	END
/**/
END
ELSE IF(ISNULL(@PrdOrdIssuePrjUsedType,0) = 2)/*(Issuing in a new project)*/
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @ProjectId = -1
/**/
END
ELSE IF(ISNULL(@PrdOrdIssuePrjUsedType,0) = 3)/*(No issuance in the absence of a project)*/
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@ProjectId,0) = 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @ProjectId = -2
	/**/
	END
/**/
END
/*----------------------------*/
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT 
		 @ProjectId [ProjectId]
		,[ProductID]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [ProjectTitleGroup] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectTitleGroups] PTG WHERE PTG.[ProjectTitleGroupId] = [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleGroupId]) [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleGroup]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [ProjectTitle] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectTitles] PT WHERE PT.[ProjectTitleID] = [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleId]) [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitle]
		,[PrdOrdIssuePrjPostFix]		
	FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] P
	WHERE P.ProductID = @ProductId	
/**/
END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT 
		 @ProjectId [ProjectId]
		,[ProductID]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [ProjectTitleGroup] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectTitleGroups_Lang] PTG WHERE PTG.[ProjectTitleGroupId] = [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleGroupId] AND PTG.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleGroup]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [ProjectTitle] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectTitles_Lang] PT WHERE PT.[ProjectTitleID] = [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleId] AND PT.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitle]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [PrdOrdIssuePrjPostFix] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts_Lang] PLN WHERE PLN.[ProductID] = P.[ProductID] AND PLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdOrdIssuePrjPostFix]		
	FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] P
	WHERE P.ProductID = @ProductId	
/**/
END
/*----------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END

/*RZ14020915*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspCheckPreOrderHaveStockSupply]
/* 
	exec WSspCheckPreOrderHaveStockSupply 3075
*/		
(
@PreOrderId INT 
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(ISNULL((
		ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL(pordIQuantity,0)) 
				FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems 
				WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderId				
				AND pordIPrdId NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
								      FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
								      WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )				
				),0)
		-
		ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL([stsupIQuantity],0))      
				FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems] SI
				WHERE SI.stsupIPreOrderId = @PreOrderId			
			    AND SI.stsupIPrdId NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
										  FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
										  WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )	
			    AND ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(ST.stsupIsCanceled,0) FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupply] ST WHERE ST.StockSupplyId = SI.StockSupplyId),0) = 0),0)
		),0) = 0
	)
	BEGIN
		SELECT 1 AS Result
	END
	ELSE /*��� �ǘ��� �ѐ� ����� ������ ����� �� ������ �� ����� ����� ��� �ǘ��� ���� �� �ѐ� ���� ���� */
	/*���� ���� ���� �� ����� ��� �ǘ��� ���� �ѐ� �� ���� ����*/
	BEGIN
		SELECT 0 AS Result
	END	
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[WSspCheckStockSupplyHaveBuyOrder]





/*RZ14020918*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspCheckStockSupplyHaveBuyOrder]
/* 
	exec WSspCheckStockSupplyHaveBuyOrder 20
*/		
(
@StockSupplyId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(ISNULL((
		ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL([stsupIQuantity],0)) 
				FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems]
				WHERE [StockSupplyId] = @StockSupplyId				
				AND [stsupIPrdId] NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
										 FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
										 WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )				
				),0)
		-
		ISNULL((SELECT 
			 (SUM(ISNULL([bordIQuantity],0))) 					
		FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems]  
		WHERE [BuyOrderItemId] IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL(bordItems.[value],'0') AS INT)
								  FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems] 
								  CROSS APPLY ((SELECT [value] FROM STRING_SPLIT([stsupIBuyOrderItemIds], ',') L WHERE CAST(ISNULL([value],'0') AS INT) <> 0)) AS bordItems
								  WHERE [StockSupplyId] = @StockSupplyId 
								  AND ISNULL([stsupIBuyOrderItemIds],'') <>'')),0)
		),0) = 0
	)
	BEGIN
		SELECT 1 AS Result
	END
	ELSE /*no buyorder or no enough*/
	BEGIN
		SELECT 0 AS Result
	END	
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[WSspCheckStockSupplyHaveBuyOrderByItemIds]







/*RZ14020921*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspCheckStockSupplyHaveBuyOrderByItemIds]
/* 
	exec WSspCheckStockSupplyHaveBuyOrderByItemIds '34,35'
*/		
(
@StockSupplyItemIds NVARCHAR(4000)
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(ISNULL((
		ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL([stsupIQuantity],0)) 
				FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems]
				WHERE [StockSupplyItemId] IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString](@StockSupplyItemIds,','))				
				AND [stsupIPrdId] NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
										 FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
										 WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )				
				),0)
		-
		ISNULL((SELECT 
			 (SUM(ISNULL([bordIQuantity],0))) 					
		FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems]  
		WHERE [BuyOrderItemId] IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL(bordItems.[value],'0') AS INT)
								  FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems] 
								  CROSS APPLY ((SELECT [value] FROM STRING_SPLIT([stsupIBuyOrderItemIds], ',') L WHERE CAST(ISNULL([value],'0') AS INT) <> 0)) AS bordItems
								  WHERE [StockSupplyItemId] IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString](@StockSupplyItemIds,','))	
								  AND ISNULL([stsupIBuyOrderItemIds],'') <>'')),0)
		),0) <= 0
	)
	BEGIN
		SELECT 1 AS Result
	END
	ELSE /*no buyorder or no enough*/
	BEGIN
		SELECT 0 AS Result
	END	
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[WSspDeleteAllProductsAndPriceWithDependencies]






/*RZ14001221*/
/*RZ14020419- Add delete prjPrdHistory*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspDeleteAllProductsAndPriceWithDependencies]
(		
/*
	exec WSspDeleteAllProductsAndPriceWithDependencies 15,1033,1065
*/
@PrdGroupId INT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @TmpTable TABLE (
		[ProductId] INT,
		[TypeError] NVARCHAR(50)
)

INSERT INTO @TmpTable ([ProductId])
SELECT 
	[ProductID]     
FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
WHERE [PrdGroupID] = @PrdGroupId
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @ProductId INT
DECLARE Crsr CURSOR LOCAL FOR
/**/				
	SELECT 
		[ProductID]     
	FROM @TmpTable
/**/
OPEN Crsr
FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @ProductId
WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
BEGIN
/**/
	/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
	/*------------------------- Find PreOrder,Order,BuyOrder,Doc ------------------*/
	DECLARE @CanDelete BIT = 1, @Result NVARCHAR(50) = N''

	IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] WHERE [PreOrderID] IN(SELECT I.PreOrderId 
																	 FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] I
																	 WHERE I.pordIPrdId = @ProductId)))/*Pord*/
	BEGIN
		SET @CanDelete = 0
		IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
			SET @Result = '-1'
		ELSE 
			SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-1'
	END
	/**/
	IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] WHERE [OrderID] IN(SELECT I.OrderId 
																	 FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] I
																	 WHERE I.ordIPrdId = @ProductId)))/*Ord*/
	BEGIN
		SET @CanDelete = 0
		IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
			SET @Result = '-2'
		ELSE 
			SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-2'
	END
	/**/
	IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrder] WHERE [BuyOrderID] IN(SELECT I.BuyOrderId 
																	 FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] I
																	 WHERE I.bordIPrdId = @ProductId)))/*Bord*/
	BEGIN
		SET @CanDelete = 0
		IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
			SET @Result = '-3'
		ELSE 
			SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-3'
	END
	/**/
	IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] WHERE [DocID] IN(SELECT I.DocId 
																	 FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems] I
																	 WHERE I.PrdId = @ProductId)))/*Doc*/
	BEGIN
		SET @CanDelete = 0
		IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
			SET @Result = '-4'
		ELSE 
			SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-4'
	END	
	/*--------------------------------------------------------------------*/								
	IF(@CanDelete = 1)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		BEGIN TRY
		/**/
			BEGIN TRANSACTION 
				/*------------------------------------------------*/
				/*------------ FilesDirectory ------------*/	
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory_Lang]
				WHERE [FileDirectoryID] IN(SELECT [FileDirectoryID]     
											FROM [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory]
											WHERE [FileDirectoryTypeId] = -60
											AND [TypeOprObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
																		FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
																		WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
																		AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
																						FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
																						WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
																						OR [FOSysPartId] = 70)))

				DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory]
				WHERE [FileDirectoryTypeId] = -60
				AND [TypeOprObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
											FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
											WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
											AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
															FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
															WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
															OR [FOSysPartId] = 70))
				/*------------ DyForm ------------*/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails_Lang]
				WHERE [VerificationRequestDetailId] IN(SELECT [VerificationRequestDetailId]      
														FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails]
														WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
																						FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
																						WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 80
																						AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
																													FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
																													WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
																													AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
																																	FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
																																	WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
																																	OR [FOSysPartId] = 70))))

				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails]
				WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
												FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
												WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 80
												AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
																			FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
																			WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
																			AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
																							FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
																							WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
																							OR [FOSysPartId] = 70)))

				/**/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests_Lang]
				WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
												FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
												WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 80
												AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
																			FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
																			WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
																			AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
																							FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
																							WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
																							OR [FOSysPartId] = 70)))

				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
				WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 80
				AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
											FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
											WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
											AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
															FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
															WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
															OR [FOSysPartId] = 70))

				/**/
				DELETE FROM dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues_Lang]  
				WHERE FieldValueId IN (SELECT FormFieldValueId 
										FROM dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues] 
										WHERE MasterValueId IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
																FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues] 
																WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
																AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
																				FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
																				WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
																				OR [FOSysPartId] = 70)))
		
				DELETE FROM dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues] 
				WHERE MasterValueId IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
										FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues] 
										WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
										AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
														FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
														WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
														OR [FOSysPartId] = 70))

				/**/
				DELETE FROM dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValueDetails] 
				WHERE MasterValueId IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
										FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
										WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
										AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
														FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
														WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
														OR [FOSysPartId] = 70))
				/**/		
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
				WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
				AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
								FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
								WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
								OR [FOSysPartId] = 70)
								
				/*------------ ProductsRate ------------*/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductsRate]
				WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId

				/*------------ ProductsUserDiscount ------------*/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductsUserDiscount_Lang]
				WHERE [ProductsUserDiscountId] IN(SELECT [ProductsUserDiscountId] 
													FROM [dbo].[WSProductsUserDiscount]
													WHERE [DiscountProductId] = @ProductId)

				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductsUserDiscount]
				WHERE [DiscountProductId] = @ProductId

				/*------------ ProductRelationLimit ------------*/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductRelationLimit]
				WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId

				/*------------ LinkedProducts ------------*/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts]
				WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId
				OR [LinkedPrdId] = @ProductId

				/*------------ UserFavproductItems ------------*/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSUserFavproductItems_Lang]
				WHERE [UserFavproductItemId] IN(SELECT [UserFavproductItemId] 
													FROM [dbo].[WSUserFavproductItems]
													WHERE [FavprdIPrdId] = @ProductId)

				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSUserFavproductItems]
				WHERE [FavprdIPrdId] = @ProductId
				
				/*------------ DiscountCode ------------*/	
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSDiscountCodeHistory]
				WHERE [DiscountCodeId] IN(SELECT [DiscountCodeId]      
										  FROM [dbo].[WSDiscountCode]
										  WHERE [DisPrdId] = @ProductId)
		
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSDiscountCodeHistory]
				WHERE [DisPrdId] = @ProductId
				/**/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSDiscountCode_Lang]
				WHERE [DiscountCodeId] IN(SELECT [DiscountCodeId]      
										  FROM [dbo].[WSDiscountCode]
										  WHERE [DisPrdId] = @ProductId)

				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSDiscountCode]
				WHERE [DisPrdId] = @ProductId
				
				/*------------ CmsHomePageSectionItems ------------*/	
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmCmsHomePageSectionItems]
				WHERE [SectionItemPrdId] = @ProductId
				
				/*------------ ProductSpecification ------------*/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductSpecification_Lang]
				WHERE [ProductSpecificationID] IN(SELECT [ProductSpecificationID] 
													FROM [dbo].[WSProductSpecification]
													WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId)

				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductSpecification]
				WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId
				
				/*------------ ProductGallery ------------*/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductGallery]
				WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId


				/*------------ ProductPicture ------------*/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductPicture_Lang]
				WHERE [PrdPictureID] IN(SELECT [PrdPictureID] 
										FROM [dbo].[WSProductPicture]
										WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId)

				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductPicture]
				WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId

				/*------------ ProductCost ------------*/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductCost_Lang]
				WHERE [ProductCostID] IN(SELECT [ProductCostID] 
										FROM [dbo].[WSProductCost]
										WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId)

				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductCost]
				WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId

				/*------------ ProductPrice ------------*/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductPrice_Lang]
				WHERE [PrdPriceID] IN(SELECT [PrdPriceID] 
										FROM [dbo].[WSProductPrice]
										WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId)

				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductPrice]
				WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId				
				/*------------ CrmProjectProductHistory ------------*/
				/*RZ14020419- Add delete prjPrdHistory*/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectProductHistory_Lang]
				WHERE [ProjectProductHistoryId] IN(SELECT [ProjectProductHistoryId]     
										FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectProductHistory]
										WHERE [PrjPrdHProductId] = @ProductId)

				DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectProductHistory]
				WHERE [PrjPrdHProductId] = @ProductId
				/*RZ14020419- End*/
				/*------------ Produc ------------*/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProducts_Lang]
				WHERE [ProductID] = @ProductId

				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
				WHERE [ProductID] = @ProductId
				/*--------------------------------------------*/
				SET @Result = '1'
			COMMIT
		/**/
		END TRY
		BEGIN CATCH
		/**/
			IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
				ROLLBACK
			SET @Result = '0'
		/**/
		END CATCH
	/**/
	END
	/*----------------------------------------------------------------*/
	UPDATE @TmpTable 
	SET [TypeError] = @Result
	WHERE [ProductId] = @ProductId					
	/*-----------------------------------------------------*/														    				 					
/**/	
FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @ProductId
END
CLOSE Crsr
DEALLOCATE Crsr
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*========================================================*/
/*============= Return list of product not deleted ===============*/

IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT 
		 [ProductID]
		,[PrdName]
		,[PrdCode]
		,(SELECT [TypeError] FROM @TmpTable TM WHERE TM.[ProductID] = P.[ProductID]) [TypeError]
	FROM  [dbo].[WSProducts] P  
	WHERE P.[ProductID] IN(SELECT [ProductID] FROM @TmpTable)	
	AND P.[ProductID] NOT IN(SELECT TM.[ProductID] 
							 FROM @TmpTable TM
							 WHERE ISNULL([TypeError],'') = '1')
/**/
END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT 
		 [ProductID]
		,(SELECT PLN.[PrdName] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts_Lang] PLN WHERE PLN.ProductID = P.ProductID AND PLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdName]
		,[PrdCode]
		,(SELECT [TypeError] FROM @TmpTable TM WHERE TM.[ProductID] = P.[ProductID]) [TypeError]
	FROM  [dbo].[WSProducts] P  
	WHERE P.[ProductID] IN(SELECT [ProductID] FROM @TmpTable)	
	AND P.[ProductID] NOT IN(SELECT TM.[ProductID] 
							 FROM @TmpTable TM
							 WHERE ISNULL([TypeError],'') = '1')
/**/
END
/*========================================================*/
/**/
END








GO
-- [dbo].[WSspDeleteAllProductsWithDependencies]







/*RZ14001221*/
/*RZ14020419- Add delete prjPrdHistory*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspDeleteAllProductsWithDependencies]
(		
/*
	exec WSspDeleteAllProductsWithDependencies 15,1033,1065
*/
@PrdGroupId INT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @TmpTable TABLE (
		[ProductId] INT,
		[TypeError] NVARCHAR(50)
)

INSERT INTO @TmpTable ([ProductId])
SELECT 
	[ProductID]     
FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
WHERE [PrdGroupID] = @PrdGroupId
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @ProductId INT
DECLARE Crsr CURSOR LOCAL FOR
/**/				
	SELECT 
		[ProductID]     
	FROM @TmpTable
/**/
OPEN Crsr
FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @ProductId
WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
BEGIN
/**/
	/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
	/*------------------------- Find PreOrder,Order,BuyOrder,Doc ------------------*/
	DECLARE @CanDelete BIT = 1, @Result NVARCHAR(50) = N''

	IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] WHERE [PreOrderID] IN(SELECT I.PreOrderId 
																	 FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] I
																	 WHERE I.pordIPrdId = @ProductId)))/*Pord*/
	BEGIN
		SET @CanDelete = 0
		IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
			SET @Result = '-1'
		ELSE 
			SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-1'
	END
	/**/
	IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] WHERE [OrderID] IN(SELECT I.OrderId 
																	 FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] I
																	 WHERE I.ordIPrdId = @ProductId)))/*Ord*/
	BEGIN
		SET @CanDelete = 0
		IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
			SET @Result = '-2'
		ELSE 
			SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-2'
	END
	/**/
	IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrder] WHERE [BuyOrderID] IN(SELECT I.BuyOrderId 
																	 FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] I
																	 WHERE I.bordIPrdId = @ProductId)))/*Bord*/
	BEGIN
		SET @CanDelete = 0
		IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
			SET @Result = '-3'
		ELSE 
			SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-3'
	END
	/**/
	IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] WHERE [DocID] IN(SELECT I.DocId 
																	 FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems] I
																	 WHERE I.PrdId = @ProductId)))/*Doc*/
	BEGIN
		SET @CanDelete = 0
		IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
			SET @Result = '-4'
		ELSE 
			SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-4'
	END
	/**/
	IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSProductCost] WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId))/*PrdCost*/
	BEGIN
		SET @CanDelete = 0
		IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
			SET @Result = '-5'
		ELSE 
			SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-5'
	END
	/**/
	IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSProductPrice] WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId))/*PrdPrice*/
	BEGIN
		SET @CanDelete = 0
		IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
			SET @Result = '-6'
		ELSE 
			SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-6'
	END
	/*--------------------------------------------------------------------*/								
	IF(@CanDelete = 1)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		BEGIN TRY
		/**/
			BEGIN TRANSACTION 
				/*------------------------------------------------*/
				/*------------ FilesDirectory ------------*/	
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory_Lang]
				WHERE [FileDirectoryID] IN(SELECT [FileDirectoryID]     
											FROM [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory]
											WHERE [FileDirectoryTypeId] = -60
											AND [TypeOprObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
																		FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
																		WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
																		AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
																						FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
																						WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
																						OR [FOSysPartId] = 70)))

				DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory]
				WHERE [FileDirectoryTypeId] = -60
				AND [TypeOprObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
											FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
											WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
											AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
															FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
															WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
															OR [FOSysPartId] = 70))
				/*------------ DyForm ------------*/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails_Lang]
				WHERE [VerificationRequestDetailId] IN(SELECT [VerificationRequestDetailId]      
														FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails]
														WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
																						FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
																						WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 80
																						AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
																													FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
																													WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
																													AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
																																	FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
																																	WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
																																	OR [FOSysPartId] = 70))))

				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails]
				WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
												FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
												WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 80
												AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
																			FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
																			WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
																			AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
																							FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
																							WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
																							OR [FOSysPartId] = 70)))

				/**/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests_Lang]
				WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
												FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
												WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 80
												AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
																			FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
																			WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
																			AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
																							FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
																							WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
																							OR [FOSysPartId] = 70)))

				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
				WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 80
				AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
											FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
											WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
											AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
															FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
															WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70

										FROM dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues] 
										WHERE MasterValueId IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
																FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]

																AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]

																				OR [FOSysPartId] = 70)))

										FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues] 
										WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
										AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
														FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
														WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
														OR [FOSysPartId] = 70))

				/**/
				DELETE FROM dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValueDetails] 
				WHERE MasterValueId IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
										FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
										WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
										AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
														FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
														WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
														OR [FOSysPartId] = 70))
				/**/		
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
				WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
				AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
								FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
								WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
								OR [FOSysPartId] = 70)
								
				/*------------ ProductsRate ------------*/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductsRate]
				WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId

				/*------------ ProductsUserDiscount ------------*/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductsUserDiscount_Lang]
				WHERE [ProductsUserDiscountId] IN(SELECT [ProductsUserDiscountId] 
													FROM [dbo].[WSProductsUserDiscount]
													WHERE [DiscountProductId] = @ProductId)

				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductsUserDiscount]
				WHERE [DiscountProductId] = @ProductId

				/*------------ ProductRelationLimit ------------*/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductRelationLimit]
				WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId

				/*------------ LinkedProducts ------------*/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts]
				WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId
				OR [LinkedPrdId] = @ProductId

				/*------------ UserFavproductItems ------------*/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSUserFavproductItems_Lang]
				WHERE [UserFavproductItemId] IN(SELECT [UserFavproductItemId] 
													FROM [dbo].[WSUserFavproductItems]
													WHERE [FavprdIPrdId] = @ProductId)

				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSUserFavproductItems]
				WHERE [FavprdIPrdId] = @ProductId
				
				/*------------ DiscountCode ------------*/	
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSDiscountCodeHistory]
				WHERE [DiscountCodeId] IN(SELECT [DiscountCodeId]      
										  FROM [dbo].[WSDiscountCode]
										  WHERE [DisPrdId] = @ProductId)
		
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSDiscountCodeHistory]
				WHERE [DisPrdId] = @ProductId
				/**/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSDiscountCode_Lang]
				WHERE [DiscountCodeId] IN(SELECT [DiscountCodeId]      
										  FROM [dbo].[WSDiscountCode]

				WHERE [DisPrdId] = @ProductId
				
				/*------------ CmsHomePageSectionItems ------------*/

				WHERE [SectionItemPrdId] = @ProductId
				
				/*------------ ProductSpecification ------------*/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductSpecification_Lang]
				WHERE [ProductSpecificationID] IN(SELECT [ProductSpecificationID] 
													FROM [dbo].[WSProductSpecification]

				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductSpecification]

				/*------------ ProductGallery ------------*/

										FROM [dbo].[WSProductPicture]

				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductPicture]
				WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId
				/*------------ CrmProjectProductHistory ------------*/
				/*RZ14020419- Add delete prjPrdHistory*/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectProductHistory_Lang]
				WHERE [ProjectProductHistoryId] IN(SELECT [ProjectProductHistoryId]     
													FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectProductHistory]
													WHERE [PrjPrdHProductId] = @ProductId)

				DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectProductHistory]
				WHERE [PrjPrdHProductId] = @ProductId
				/*RZ14020419- End*/
				/*------------ Produc ------------*/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProducts_Lang]
				WHERE [ProductID] = @ProductId

				DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
				WHERE [ProductID] = @ProductId
				/*--------------------------------------------*/
				SET @Result = '1'
			COMMIT
		/**/
		END TRY
		BEGIN CATCH
		/**/
			IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
				ROLLBACK
			SET @Result = '0'
		/**/
		END CATCH
	/**/
	END
	/*----------------------------------------------------------------*/
	UPDATE @TmpTable 
	SET [TypeError] = @Result
	WHERE [ProductId] = @ProductId					
	/*-----------------------------------------------------*/														    				 					
/**/	
FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @ProductId
END
CLOSE Crsr
DEALLOCATE Crsr
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*========================================================*/
/*============= Return list of product not deleted ===============*/

IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT 
		 [ProductID]
		,[PrdName]
		,[PrdCode]
		,(SELECT [TypeError] FROM @TmpTable TM WHERE TM.[ProductID] = P.[ProductID]) [TypeError]
	FROM  [dbo].[WSProducts] P  
	WHERE P.[ProductID] IN(SELECT [ProductID] FROM @TmpTable)	
	AND P.[ProductID] NOT IN(SELECT TM.[ProductID] 
							 FROM @TmpTable TM
							 WHERE ISNULL([TypeError],'') = '1')
/**/
END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT 
		 [ProductID]
		,(SELECT PLN.[PrdName] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts_Lang] PLN WHERE PLN.ProductID = P.ProductID AND PLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdName]
		,[PrdCode]
		,(SELECT [TypeError] FROM @TmpTable TM WHERE TM.[ProductID] = P.[ProductID]) [TypeError]
	FROM  [dbo].[WSProducts] P  
	WHERE P.[ProductID] IN(SELECT [ProductID] FROM @TmpTable)	
	AND P.[ProductID] NOT IN(SELECT TM.[ProductID] 
							 FROM @TmpTable TM
							 WHERE ISNULL([TypeError],'') = '1')
/**/
END
/*========================================================*/
/**/
END
