-- [dbo].[WSspDeleteBuyOrderItemsAll]





/*NKR14001104*/
/*RZ14020925- Add remove bordid and bordItem from stockSupply*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[WSspDeleteBuyOrderItemsAll]
(
	/*
		EXEC [WSspDeleteBuyOrderItemsAll] 332
	*/
	@BuyOrderId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
	/**/
	DECLARE @Result INT
	/**/
	BEGIN TRY
		/**/
		/*---------------------------------------*/
		/*RZ14020925- Add remove bordid and bordItem from stockSupply*/

		DECLARE @CrsrStockSupplyItemId INT = NULL,@CrsrBordIds NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,@CrsrBordItemIds NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL
		DECLARE @bordIdTmp TABLE(
			bordId INT
		)
		DECLARE @bordItemdTmp TABLE(
			bordItemId INT
		)
		DECLARE CrsrUpStsupItem CURSOR LOCAL FOR
		/**/	
			SELECT 
				 [StockSupplyItemId]
				,[stsupIBuyOrderIds]
				,[stsupIBuyOrderItemIds]					
			FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems]
			WHERE ISNULL([stsupIBuyOrderIds],'') <> ''
			AND @BuyOrderId IN (SELECT 
									CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) 
								FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString]([stsupIBuyOrderIds],',')
								WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> 0)															   								 							  		
		/**/
		OPEN CrsrUpStsupItem
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrUpStsupItem INTO @CrsrStockSupplyItemId,@CrsrBordIds,@CrsrBordItemIds
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*-----------------------------------------------------*/			
			DELETE FROM @bordIdTmp
			DELETE FROM @bordItemdTmp
			/**/
			IF(ISNULL(@CrsrBordIds,'') <> '')
			BEGIN
			/**/
				INSERT INTO @bordIdTmp (bordId)
				SELECT 
					CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) 
				FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString](@CrsrBordIds,',')
				WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> 0
				AND CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> @BuyOrderId		
				
				SET @CrsrBordIds = (STUFF((SELECT ',' + CAST(ISNULL(bordId, '0') AS NVARCHAR(50))
									FROM  @bordIdTmp
									WHERE ISNULL(bordId, '0') <> 0 							
									FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, ''))
			/**/
			END
			/**/
			IF(ISNULL(@CrsrBordItemIds,'') <> '')
			BEGIN
			/**/
				INSERT INTO @bordItemdTmp (bordItemId)
				SELECT 
					CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) 
				FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString](@CrsrBordItemIds,',')
				WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> 0
				AND CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) NOT IN(SELECT BuyOrderItemId FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] WHERE BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId)	
				
				SET @CrsrBordItemIds = (STUFF((SELECT ',' + CAST(ISNULL(bordItemId, '0') AS NVARCHAR(50))
										FROM  @bordItemdTmp
										WHERE ISNULL(bordItemId, '0') <> 0 							
										FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, ''))
			/**/
			END
			/**/
			UPDATE [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems]
			SET [stsupIBuyOrderIds] = @CrsrBordIds
				,[stsupIBuyOrderItemIds] = @CrsrBordItemIds
			WHERE [StockSupplyItemId] = @CrsrStockSupplyItemId			
			/*-----------------------------------------------------*/														    				 					
		/**/	
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrUpStsupItem INTO @CrsrStockSupplyItemId,@CrsrBordIds,@CrsrBordItemIds
		END
		CLOSE CrsrUpStsupItem
		DEALLOCATE CrsrUpStsupItem
		/*RZ14020925- End*/
		/*---------------------------------------*/
		DELETE WSBuyOrderItems_Lang WHERE BuyOrderItemId IN (SELECT BOI.BuyOrderItemId FROM WSBuyOrderItems BOI WHERE BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId)
		DELETE WSBuyOrderItems WHERE BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId
		/**/
		SET @Result = 1
		/**/
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		SET @Result = 0
	END CATCH
	/**/
	SELECT @Result AS [Result]
	/**/
END

-- [dbo].[WSspDeleteBuyOrderItemWithLinkedPrd]





/*RZ14000525*/
/*RZ14020925- Add remove bordid and bordItem from stockSupply*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspDeleteBuyOrderItemWithLinkedPrd]
(		
/*
	exec WSspDeleteBuyOrderItemWithLinkedPrd 333,1736
*/
@BuyOrderId INT,
@BuyOrderItemId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 
		/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
		/*------------------------ Delete items by linked -----------------*/
		DECLARE @ProductId INT
		SET @ProductId = (SELECT TOP 1 [bordIPrdId] FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] WHERE [BuyOrderItemID] = @BuyOrderItemId) 
				
		DELETE [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItemSerials_Lang] 
		WHERE BuyOrderItemSerialID IN (SELECT 
											BuyOrderItemSerialID 
									   FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItemSerials]
									   WHERE BuyOrderItemId IN (SELECT 
																	I.BuyOrderItemID 
																 FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] I 
																 WHERE BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId
																 AND [bordIPrdId] IN(SELECT 
																						(CASE WHEN PrdId = @ProductId 
																							THEN LinkedPrdId
																							ELSE PrdId									
																						END) [LinkedPrdId]			 
																					 FROM [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts]	
																					 WHERE ([PrdId] = @ProductId	
																					 OR [LinkedPrdId] = @ProductId)			
																					 )))

		DELETE [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItemSerials] 
		WHERE BuyOrderItemId IN (SELECT 
									I.BuyOrderItemID 
								  FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] I 
								  WHERE BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId
								  AND [bordIPrdId] IN(SELECT 
														(CASE WHEN PrdId = @ProductId 
															THEN LinkedPrdId
															ELSE PrdId									
														END) [LinkedPrdId]			 
													 FROM [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts]	
													 WHERE ([PrdId] = @ProductId	
													 OR [LinkedPrdId] = @ProductId)			
													 ))
		/*-----------------------*/
		/*---------------------------------------*/
		/*RZ14020925- Add remove bordid and bordItem from stockSupply*/
		DECLARE @CrsrStockSupplyItemId INT = NULL,@CrsrBordIds NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,@CrsrBordItemIds NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL		
		DECLARE @bordIdTmp TABLE(
			bordId INT
		)
		DECLARE @bordItemdTmp TABLE(
			bordItemId INT
		)
		DECLARE CrsrUpStsupItem CURSOR LOCAL FOR
		/**/	
			SELECT 
				 [StockSupplyItemId]
				,[stsupIBuyOrderIds]
				,[stsupIBuyOrderItemIds]					
			FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems]
			WHERE ISNULL([stsupIBuyOrderIds],'') <> ''
			AND @BuyOrderId IN (SELECT 
									CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) 
								FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString]([stsupIBuyOrderIds],',')
								WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> 0)															   								 							  		
		/**/
		OPEN CrsrUpStsupItem
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrUpStsupItem INTO @CrsrStockSupplyItemId,@CrsrBordIds,@CrsrBordItemIds
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*-----------------------------------------------------*/			
			DELETE FROM @bordIdTmp
			DELETE FROM @bordItemdTmp
			/**/
			DECLARE @RemoveBord BIT = 0
			/**/
			IF(ISNULL(@CrsrBordItemIds,'') <> '')
			BEGIN
			/**/
				INSERT INTO @bordItemdTmp (bordItemId)
				SELECT 
					CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) 
				FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString](@CrsrBordItemIds,',')
				WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> 0
				AND CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) NOT IN(SELECT BuyOrderItemId 
															FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] 
															WHERE BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId
															AND [bordIPrdId] IN (SELECT 
																					 (CASE WHEN PrdId = @ProductId 
																						   THEN LinkedPrdId
																						   ELSE PrdId									
																					 END) [LinkedPrdId]			 
																				FROM [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts]	
																				WHERE ([PrdId] = @ProductId	
																				OR 	[LinkedPrdId] = @ProductId)			
																				)
															UNION 
															SELECT @BuyOrderItemId)	
				
				SET @CrsrBordItemIds = (STUFF((SELECT ',' + CAST(ISNULL(bordItemId, '0') AS NVARCHAR(50))
										FROM  @bordItemdTmp
										WHERE ISNULL(bordItemId, '0') <> 0 							
										FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, ''))

				/**/
				IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) bordItemId FROM @bordItemdTmp WHERE bordItemId IN(SELECT BuyOrderItemId FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] WHERE BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId )),0) = 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @RemoveBord = 1
				END				
			/**/
			END
			/**/
			IF(ISNULL(@CrsrBordIds,'') <> '' AND ISNULL(@RemoveBord,0) = 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				INSERT INTO @bordIdTmp (bordId)
				SELECT 
					CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) 
				FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString](@CrsrBordIds,',')
				WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> 0
				AND CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> @BuyOrderId		
				
				SET @CrsrBordIds = (STUFF((SELECT ',' + CAST(ISNULL(bordId, '0') AS NVARCHAR(50))
									FROM  @bordIdTmp
									WHERE ISNULL(bordId, '0') <> 0 							
									FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, ''))

				
			/**/
			END
			/**/
			
			UPDATE [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems]
			SET [stsupIBuyOrderIds] = @CrsrBordIds
				,[stsupIBuyOrderItemIds] = @CrsrBordItemIds
			WHERE [StockSupplyItemId] = @CrsrStockSupplyItemId			
			/*-----------------------------------------------------*/														    				 					
		/**/	
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrUpStsupItem INTO @CrsrStockSupplyItemId,@CrsrBordIds,@CrsrBordItemIds
		END
		CLOSE CrsrUpStsupItem
		DEALLOCATE CrsrUpStsupItem
		/*RZ14020925- End*/
		/*---------------------------------------*/
		/*-----------------------*/

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems_Lang]  
		WHERE BuyOrderItemID IN (SELECT I.BuyOrderItemID FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] I 
								WHERE BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId
								AND [bordIPrdId] IN(SELECT 
															(CASE WHEN PrdId = @ProductId 
																THEN LinkedPrdId
																ELSE PrdId									
															END) [LinkedPrdId]			 
													FROM [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts]	
													WHERE ([PrdId] = @ProductId	
													OR 	[LinkedPrdId] = @ProductId)			
													))

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] 
		WHERE BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId
		AND [bordIPrdId] IN (SELECT 
								 (CASE WHEN PrdId = @ProductId 
									   THEN LinkedPrdId
									   ELSE PrdId									
								 END) [LinkedPrdId]			 
							FROM [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts]	
							WHERE ([PrdId] = @ProductId	
							OR 	[LinkedPrdId] = @ProductId)			
							)
		
		/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
		/*---------------------- Delete main item -----------------------*/
		
		DELETE [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItemSerials_Lang] 
		WHERE BuyOrderItemSerialID IN (SELECT 
										BuyOrderItemSerialID 
									FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItemSerials]
									WHERE BuyOrderItemId = @BuyOrderItemId)
		DELETE [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItemSerials] WHERE BuyOrderItemId = @BuyOrderItemId		
		/*-----------------------*/

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems_Lang]  WHERE BuyOrderItemID = @BuyOrderItemId
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems]  WHERE BuyOrderItemID = @BuyOrderItemId

		/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
		SELECT 1 AS Result
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	SELECT 0 AS Result
END CATCH
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[WSspDeleteCostSheetWithItemsAndFieldValues]










/*RZ14000215*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[WSspDeleteCostSheetWithItemsAndFieldValues] 
(		
/*
	EXEC WSspDeleteCostsheetWithItemsAndFieldValues 84
*/
@CostSheetId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
DECLARE @RecordNumber AS BIGINT = 0
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 
		/**/
		DELETE [dbo].[WSCostSheetItems] WHERE [CostSheetId] = @CostSheetId
		DELETE [dbo].[WSCostSheetDyFieldValues] WHERE [CostSheetId] = @CostSheetId
		/**/
		DELETE [dbo].[WSCostSheets_Lang]  WHERE [CostSheetId] = @CostSheetId
        DELETE [dbo].[WSCostSheets]  WHERE [CostSheetId] = @CostSheetId
    COMMIT
	SELECT 1 AS Result,@RecordNumber RecordNumber
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
/**/
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	/*------------------------- Get reason ------------------------------------*/
	IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 BuyOrderID 
			   FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrder] 
			   WHERE bordCostSheetId = @CostSheetId),0) > 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/		
		SET @RecordNumber = (SELECT TOP 1 bordNumber 
							FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrder] 
							WHERE bordCostSheetId = @CostSheetId)		
		SELECT -1 AS Result,@RecordNumber RecordNumber/*BuyOrder*/
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 BuyOrderId 
					FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] 
					WHERE bordICostSheetItemId = @CostSheetId),0) > 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @RecordNumber = (SELECT TOP 1 bordNumber 
							FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrder] 
							WHERE bordCostSheetId = (SELECT TOP 1 BuyOrderId 
													 FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] 
													 WHERE bordICostSheetItemId = @CostSheetId))
		SELECT -1 AS Result,@RecordNumber RecordNumber/*BuyOrder*/
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 PreOrderId 
					FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] 
					WHERE pordICostSheetItemId IN(SELECT CostSheetItemId 
												  FROM [dbo].[WSCostSheetItems] 
												  WHERE CostSheetId = @CostSheetId) ),0) > 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @RecordNumber = (SELECT  TOP 1 (SELECT TOP 1 pordNumber FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] PO WHERE PO.PreOrderId = POI.PreOrderId) pordNumber
							 FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] POI
							 WHERE pordICostSheetItemId IN(SELECT CostSheetItemId 
														   FROM [dbo].[WSCostSheetItems] 
														   WHERE CostSheetId = @CostSheetId) )

		SELECT -2 AS Result,@RecordNumber RecordNumber/*PreOrder*/
	/**/
	END
	ELSE 
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT 0 AS Result,@RecordNumber RecordNumber
	/**/
	END	
/**/
END CATCH
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[WSspDeleteDocItemsAll]





/*RZ14001215*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspDeleteDocItemsAll]
(		
/*
	exec WSspDeleteDocItemsAll 559
*/
@DocId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 
		/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/						
		DELETE [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials_Lang] 
		WHERE DocItemSerialID IN (SELECT 
										DocItemSerialID 
									FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials]
									WHERE ItemId IN(SELECT DocItemID
													FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems]  
													WHERE DocID = @DocId))

		DELETE [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] WHERE ItemId IN(SELECT DocItemID
														FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems]  
														WHERE DocID = @DocId)	
		
		/*-----------------------*/

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems_Lang]  
		WHERE DocItemID IN(SELECT DocItemID
							FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems]  
							WHERE DocID = @DocId)

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems]  WHERE DocID = @DocId

		/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
		SELECT 1 AS Result
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	SELECT 0 AS Result
END CATCH
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[WSspDeleteDocItemWithLinkedPrd]






/*RZ14000525*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspDeleteDocItemWithLinkedPrd]
(		
/*
	exec WSspDeleteDocItemWithLinkedPrd 559,2200
*/
@DocId INT,
@DocItemId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 
		/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
		/*------------------------ Delete items by linked -----------------*/
		DECLARE @ProductId INT
		SET @ProductId = (SELECT TOP 1 [PrdId] FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems] WHERE [DocItemID] = @DocItemId) 
				
		DELETE [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials_Lang] 
		WHERE DocItemSerialID IN (SELECT 
											DocItemSerialID 
									   FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials]
									   WHERE ItemId IN (SELECT 
																	I.DocItemID 
																 FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems] I 
																 WHERE DocId = @DocId
																 AND [PrdId] IN(SELECT 
																						(CASE WHEN PrdId = @ProductId 
																							THEN LinkedPrdId
																							ELSE PrdId									
																						END) [LinkedPrdId]			 
																					 FROM [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts]	
																					 WHERE ([PrdId] = @ProductId	
																					 OR [LinkedPrdId] = @ProductId)			
																					 )))

		DELETE [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] 
		WHERE ItemId IN (SELECT 
									I.DocItemID 
								  FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems] I 
								  WHERE DocId = @DocId
								  AND [PrdId] IN(SELECT 
														(CASE WHEN PrdId = @ProductId 
															THEN LinkedPrdId
															ELSE PrdId									
														END) [LinkedPrdId]			 
													 FROM [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts]	
													 WHERE ([PrdId] = @ProductId	
													 OR [LinkedPrdId] = @ProductId)			
													 ))

		/*-----------------------*/

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems_Lang]  
		WHERE DocItemID IN (SELECT I.DocItemID FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems] I 
								WHERE DocId = @DocId
								AND [PrdId] IN(SELECT 
															(CASE WHEN PrdId = @ProductId 
																THEN LinkedPrdId
																ELSE PrdId									
															END) [LinkedPrdId]			 
													FROM [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts]	
													WHERE ([PrdId] = @ProductId	
													OR 	[LinkedPrdId] = @ProductId)			
													))

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems] 
		WHERE DocId = @DocId
		AND [PrdId] IN (SELECT 
								 (CASE WHEN PrdId = @ProductId 
									   THEN LinkedPrdId
									   ELSE PrdId									
								 END) [LinkedPrdId]			 
							FROM [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts]	
							WHERE ([PrdId] = @ProductId	
							OR 	[LinkedPrdId] = @ProductId)			
							)
		
		/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
		/*---------------------- Delete main item -----------------------*/
		
		DELETE [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials_Lang] 
		WHERE DocItemSerialID IN (SELECT 
										DocItemSerialID 
									FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials]
									WHERE ItemId = @DocItemId)
		DELETE [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] WHERE ItemId = @DocItemId		
		
		/*-----------------------*/

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems_Lang]  WHERE DocItemID = @DocItemId
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems]  WHERE DocItemID = @DocItemId

		/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
		SELECT 1 AS Result
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	SELECT 0 AS Result
END CATCH
/**/
END






GO
-- [dbo].[WSspDeleteLinkedProduct]






/*NKR991121*/
CREATE procedure [dbo].[WSspDeleteLinkedProduct]
(
	/*
		exec WSspDeleteLinkedProduct 1024,1420
	*/
	@ProductId int, 
	@LinkedProductId int
)
AS

	WHERE (PrdId = @ProductId and LinkedPrdId = @LinkedProductId) OR 
		  (PrdId = @LinkedProductId and LinkedPrdId = @ProductId)
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[WSspDeleteOrder]






/*ADBF9002*/
/*RZ960130 for multi language*/
/*RZ960218 change condition for delete doc, because this new 
design in doc orderId unic in wsdoc, so delete wsdoc with condition orderid is enough*/
/*RZ960428- Delete doc_lang*/
/*RZ14000226- Delete multiple docs for a order*/
/*RZ14001012- Add OrderCommissions*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[WSspDeleteOrder] /*--*/
(		
/*
	EXEC WSspDeleteOrder 2, 16826 , 3, '1390'
*/
@Mode INT,
@OrderId INT,
@UserId INT,
@Date VARCHAR(20)
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
IF @Mode = 1	/*--Delete Order*/
BEGIN
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 
			
/*--SELECT * FROM */
		DELETE dbo.WSOrderItemSerials_Lang 
			WHERE OrderItemSerialID IN 
			(SELECT OrderItemSerialID FROM dbo.WSOrderItemSerials OIS WHERE OIS.OrderItemId IN 
			(SELECT OrderItemId FROM dbo.WSOrderItems OI WHERE OI.OrderId = @OrderId))
		DELETE dbo.WSOrderItemSerials
			WHERE OrderItemId IN (SELECT OrderItemId FROM dbo.WSOrderItems WHERE OrderId = @OrderId)	
/*--*/
/*--SELECT * FROM */
		DELETE dbo.WSOrderItems_Lang WHERE OrderItemID IN 
			(SELECT OrderItemID FROM dbo.WSOrderItems OI WHERE OI.OrderId = @OrderId)
		DELETE dbo.WSOrderItems
			WHERE OrderId = @OrderId
/*--*/
/*RZ14001012- Add OrderCommissions*/
		DELETE dbo.WSOrderCommissions_Lang WHERE [OrderCommissionId] IN 
			(SELECT [OrderCommissionId] FROM dbo.WSOrderCommissions OC WHERE OC.[CommOrderId] = @OrderId)
		DELETE dbo.WSOrderCommissions
			WHERE [CommOrderId] = @OrderId
/*RZ14001012- End*/

/*--SELECT * FROM */
		DELETE WSOrder_Lang  WHERE OrderId = @OrderId
		DELETE dbo.WSOrder WHERE OrderId = @OrderId
		Select 1 as Result
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	Select 0 as Result
END CATCH
END
/*-------------------------------------------------------
-------------------------------------------------------*/
ELSE 
IF @Mode = 2	/*--Delete Doc*/
BEGIN
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 
/*--SELECT * FROM*/ 
		DELETE dbo.WSDocItemSerials_Lang 
		WHERE DocItemSerialID IN 
			(SELECT DocItemSerialID FROM dbo.WSDocItemSerials DIS WHERE DIS.ItemId IN 
			(SELECT DocItemID FROM dbo.WSDocItems DI WHERE DI.DocId IN 
			(SELECT DocID FROM dbo.WSDoc WHERE OrderId = @OrderId)))
		DELETE dbo.WSDocItemSerials
		WHERE ItemId IN 
			(SELECT DocItemID FROM dbo.WSDocItems WHERE DocId /*=*/IN /*RZ14000226- Delete multiple docs for a order*/ 
			(SELECT DocID FROM dbo.WSDoc WHERE OrderId = @OrderId))		
/*--*/
/*--SELECT * FROM*/ 
		/*RZ960428- Delete doc_lang*/
		DELETE dbo.WSDocItems_Lang 
		WHERE DocItemID IN 
			(SELECT DocItemID FROM dbo.WSDocItems DI WHERE DI.DocId IN 
			(SELECT DocID FROM dbo.WSDoc WHERE OrderId = @OrderId))


		DELETE dbo.WSDocItems 
		WHERE DocId /*=*/IN /*RZ14000226- Delete multiple docs for a order*/  
					(SELECT DocID FROM dbo.WSDoc WHERE OrderId = @OrderId)
/*--*/
	/*RZ960428- Delete doc_lang*/
		DELETE dbo.WSDoc_Lang 
		WHERE DocID /*=*/IN /*RZ14000226- Delete multiple docs for a order*/
				 (SELECT DocID FROM dbo.WSDoc WHERE OrderId = @OrderId)

/*--SELECT * FROM */
		/*DELETE dbo.WSDoc
		WHERE OprId = 2
		AND OrderId = @OrderId*/	
		/*RZ960218 change condition for delete doc, because this new 
		design in doc orderId unic in wsdoc, so delete wsdoc with condition orderid is enough*/
		DELETE dbo.WSDoc
		WHERE OrderId = @OrderId


		Select 1 as Result
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	Select 0 as Result
END CATCH
END
/*-------------------------------------------------------
-------------------------------------------------------*/
ELSE
IF @Mode = 3	/*--Delete Doc+Order*/
BEGIN
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 
/*--SELECT * FROM*/ 
		DELETE dbo.WSDocItemSerials_Lang 
		WHERE DocItemSerialID IN 
			(SELECT DocItemSerialID FROM dbo.WSDocItemSerials DIS WHERE DIS.ItemId IN 
			(SELECT DocItemID FROM dbo.WSDocItems DI WHERE DI.DocId IN 
			(SELECT DocID FROM dbo.WSDoc WHERE OrderId = @OrderId)))
		DELETE dbo.WSDocItemSerials
		WHERE ItemId IN 
			(SELECT DocItemID FROM dbo.WSDocItems WHERE DocId /*=*/IN /*RZ14000226- Delete multiple docs for a order*/ 
			(SELECT DocID FROM dbo.WSDoc WHERE OrderId = @OrderId))		
/*--*/
/*--SELECT * FROM*/ 
		/*RZ960428- Delete doc_lang*/
		DELETE dbo.WSDocItems_Lang 
		WHERE DocItemID IN 
			(SELECT DocItemID FROM dbo.WSDocItems DI WHERE DI.DocId IN 
			(SELECT DocID FROM dbo.WSDoc WHERE OrderId = @OrderId))

		DELETE dbo.WSDocItems 
		WHERE DocId /*=*/IN /*RZ14000226- Delete multiple docs for a order*/ 
				 (SELECT DocID FROM dbo.WSDoc WHERE OrderId = @OrderId)
/*--*/
	/*RZ960428- Delete doc_lang*/
		DELETE dbo.WSDoc_Lang 
		WHERE DocID /*=*/IN /*RZ14000226- Delete multiple docs for a order*/ 
				 (SELECT DocID FROM dbo.WSDoc WHERE OrderId = @OrderId)
/*--SELECT * FROM */
		/*DELETE dbo.WSDoc
		WHERE OprId = 2
		AND OrderId = @OrderId	*/
		/*RZ960218 change condition for delete doc, because this new 
		design in doc orderId unic in wsdoc, so delete wsdoc with condition orderid is enough*/
		DELETE dbo.WSDoc
		WHERE OrderId = @OrderId
/*------------------------------------------------------*/
/*------------------------------------------------------*/
/*--SELECT * FROM */
		DELETE dbo.WSOrderItemSerials_Lang 
			WHERE OrderItemSerialID IN 
			(SELECT OrderItemSerialID FROM dbo.WSOrderItemSerials OIS WHERE OIS.OrderItemId IN 
			(SELECT OrderItemId FROM dbo.WSOrderItems OI WHERE OI.OrderId = @OrderId))
		DELETE dbo.WSOrderItemSerials
			WHERE OrderItemId IN (SELECT OrderItemId FROM dbo.WSOrderItems WHERE OrderId = @OrderId)	
/*--*/
/*--SELECT * FROM */
		DELETE dbo.WSOrderItems_Lang WHERE OrderItemID IN 
			(SELECT OrderItemID FROM dbo.WSOrderItems OI WHERE OI.OrderId = @OrderId)
		DELETE dbo.WSOrderItems
			WHERE OrderId = @OrderId
/*--*/
/*RZ14001012- Add OrderCommissions*/
		DELETE dbo.WSOrderCommissions_Lang WHERE [OrderCommissionId] IN 
			(SELECT [OrderCommissionId] FROM dbo.WSOrderCommissions OC WHERE OC.[CommOrderId] = @OrderId)
		DELETE dbo.WSOrderCommissions
			WHERE [CommOrderId] = @OrderId
/*RZ14001012- End*/
/*--SELECT * FROM */
		DELETE WSOrder_Lang  WHERE OrderId = @OrderId
		DELETE dbo.WSOrder WHERE OrderId = @OrderId
		
		Select 1 as Result
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	Select 0 as Result
END CATCH
END
/**/
END

(
	/*
		EXEC [WSspDeleteOrderItemsAll] 0
	*/
	@OrderId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
	/**/
	DECLARE @Result INT
	/**/
	BEGIN TRY
		/**/
		DELETE WSOrderItemSerials_Lang 
		WHERE OrderItemSerialID IN (
									  SELECT OIS.OrderItemSerialID 
									  FROM WSOrderItemSerials OIS 
									  WHERE OIS.OrderItemId IN (
																 SELECT OrderItemId 
																 FROM WSOrderItems 
																 WHERE OrderId = @OrderId
															   )
									)
		DELETE WSOrderItemSerials WHERE OrderItemId IN (SELECT OrderItemId FROM WSOrderItems WHERE OrderId = @OrderId)
		/**/
		DELETE WSOrderItems_Lang WHERE OrderItemID IN (SELECT OI.OrderItemID FROM WSOrderItems OI WHERE OrderId = @OrderId)
		DELETE WSOrderItems WHERE OrderId = @OrderId
		/**/
		SET @Result = 1
		/**/
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		SET @Result = 0
	END CATCH
	/**/
	SELECT @Result AS [Result]
	/**/
END

-- [dbo].[WSspDeleteOrderItemWithLinkedPrd]







/*RZ14000525*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspDeleteOrderItemWithLinkedPrd]
(		
/*
	exec WSspDeleteOrderItemWithLinkedPrd 17586,3033
*/
@OrderId INT,
@OrderItemId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 
		/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
		/*------------------------ Delete items by linked -----------------*/
		DECLARE @ProductId INT
		SET @ProductId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ordIPrdId] FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] WHERE [OrderItemID] = @OrderItemId) 
				
		DELETE [dbo].[WSOrderItemSerials_Lang] 
		WHERE OrderItemSerialID IN (SELECT 
										OrderItemSerialID 
									FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItemSerials]
									WHERE OrderItemId IN (SELECT 
																I.OrderItemID 
														 FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] I 
														 WHERE OrderId = @OrderId
														 AND [ordIPrdId] IN(SELECT 
																				(CASE WHEN PrdId = @ProductId 
																					THEN LinkedPrdId
																					ELSE PrdId									
																				END) [LinkedPrdId]			 
																			 FROM [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts]	
																			 WHERE ([PrdId] = @ProductId	
																			 OR [LinkedPrdId] = @ProductId)			
																			 )))

		DELETE [dbo].[WSOrderItemSerials] 
		WHERE OrderItemId IN (SELECT 
								I.OrderItemID 
							  FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] I 
							  WHERE OrderId = @OrderId
							  AND [ordIPrdId] IN(SELECT 
													(CASE WHEN PrdId = @ProductId 
														THEN LinkedPrdId
														ELSE PrdId									
													END) [LinkedPrdId]			 
												 FROM [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts]	
												 WHERE ([PrdId] = @ProductId	
												 OR [LinkedPrdId] = @ProductId)			
												 ))

		/*-----------------------*/

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems_Lang]  
		WHERE OrderItemID IN (SELECT I.OrderItemID FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] I 
								WHERE OrderId = @OrderId
								AND [ordIPrdId] IN(SELECT 
															(CASE WHEN PrdId = @ProductId 
																THEN LinkedPrdId
																ELSE PrdId									
															END) [LinkedPrdId]			 
													FROM [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts]	
													WHERE ([PrdId] = @ProductId	
													OR 	[LinkedPrdId] = @ProductId)			
													))

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] 
		WHERE OrderId = @OrderId
		AND [ordIPrdId] IN (SELECT 
								 (CASE WHEN PrdId = @ProductId 
									   THEN LinkedPrdId
									   ELSE PrdId									
								 END) [LinkedPrdId]			 
							FROM [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts]	
							WHERE ([PrdId] = @ProductId	
							OR 	[LinkedPrdId] = @ProductId)			
							)
		
		/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
		/*---------------------- Delete main item -----------------------*/
		
		DELETE [dbo].[WSOrderItemSerials_Lang] 
		WHERE OrderItemSerialID IN (SELECT 
										OrderItemSerialID 
									FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItemSerials]
									WHERE OrderItemId = @OrderItemId)
		DELETE [dbo].[WSOrderItemSerials] WHERE OrderItemId = @OrderItemId
		
		/*-----------------------*/

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems_Lang]  WHERE OrderItemID = @OrderItemId
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems]  WHERE OrderItemID = @OrderItemId

		/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
		SELECT 1 AS Result
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	SELECT 0 AS Result
END CATCH
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[WSspDeletePreOrderDiscount]






/*RZ14021223*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspDeletePreOrderDiscount]
(		
	/*
		exec [WSspDeletePreOrderDiscount] 1
	*/
@PreOrderDiscountId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 
		/*Delete User MoneyBag*/
		DECLARE @UMnBgSystemPartId INT = 20 ,@UMnBgSystemPartObjectId INT,@UMnBgDiscountStrategyId INT
		
		SELECT TOP (1)
			 @UMnBgSystemPartObjectId = [pordDisPreOrderId]
			,@UMnBgDiscountStrategyId = [pordDisDiscountStrategyId]			
		FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderDiscounts]
		WHERE [PreOrderDiscountId] = @PreOrderDiscountId
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@UMnBgDiscountStrategyId,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			EXEC [dbo].[CRMspDeleteUserMoneyBagBySysPartAndDisSt]
			@UMnBgSystemPartId = @UMnBgSystemPartId,
			@UMnBgSystemPartObjectId = @UMnBgSystemPartObjectId,
			@UMnBgDiscountStrategyId = @UMnBgDiscountStrategyId
		END		
		/**/
		DELETE [dbo].[WSPreOrderDiscounts_Lang] WHERE [PreOrderDiscountId] = @PreOrderDiscountId
        DELETE [dbo].[WSPreOrderDiscounts] WHERE [PreOrderDiscountId] = @PreOrderDiscountId
		SELECT 1 AS Result							
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	SELECT 0 AS Result
END CATCH
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[WSspDeletePreOrderDiscountAll]






/*RZ14030114*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspDeletePreOrderDiscountAll]
(		
	/*
		exec [WSspDeletePreOrderDiscountAll] 4769
	*/
@PreOrderId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 
		/*Delete User MoneyBag*/
		DECLARE @UMnBgSystemPartId INT = 20 ,@UMnBgSystemPartObjectId INT,@UMnBgDiscountStrategyId INT
		
		DECLARE CrsrDelUserMnBg CURSOR LOCAL FOR
		/**/				
			SELECT 
				 [pordDisDiscountStrategyId]			
			FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderDiscounts]
			WHERE [pordDisPreOrderId] = @PreOrderId
		/**/
		OPEN CrsrDelUserMnBg
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrDelUserMnBg INTO @UMnBgDiscountStrategyId
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@UMnBgDiscountStrategyId,0) <> 0)
			BEGIN
				EXEC [dbo].[CRMspDeleteUserMoneyBagBySysPartAndDisSt]
				@UMnBgSystemPartId = @UMnBgSystemPartId,
				@UMnBgSystemPartObjectId = @PreOrderId,
				@UMnBgDiscountStrategyId = @UMnBgDiscountStrategyId
			END								
			/*-----------------------------------------------------*/														    				 					
		/**/	
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrDelUserMnBg INTO @UMnBgDiscountStrategyId
		END
		CLOSE CrsrDelUserMnBg
		DEALLOCATE CrsrDelUserMnBg
		/*-----------------------------------------------------*/	
		/**/
		DELETE [dbo].[WSPreOrderDiscounts_Lang] 
		WHERE [PreOrderDiscountId] IN(SELECT [PreOrderDiscountId]      
									  FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderDiscounts] PD
									  WHERE PD.[pordDisPreOrderId] = @PreOrderId)
        DELETE [dbo].[WSPreOrderDiscounts] WHERE [pordDisPreOrderId] = @PreOrderId
		SELECT 1 AS Result							
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	SELECT 0 AS Result
END CATCH
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[WSspDeletePreOrderFromPreOrderIndicator]






/*RZ14020618*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[WSspDeletePreOrderFromPreOrderIndicator]
/* 
	exec WSspDeletePreOrderFromPreOrderIndicator 3, 1033, 1065 

*/
(
@UserId INT,
@LanguageID INT , /*Use in future*/
@DefaultLanguageID INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
DECLARE @IndicatorTermTxt NVARCHAR(MAX), @txt NVARCHAR(MAX)
SELECT @IndicatorTermTxt = Txt FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE TypeId = 30 AND UserId = @UserId


DECLARE @PordTbl TABLE(
	pordId INT
)
/*---------------------------*/
SET @txt = N' SELECT PreOrderId FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] PO' + @IndicatorTermTxt 
INSERT INTO @PordTbl(pordId)
EXECUTE (@txt)

DELETE FROM @PordTbl 
WHERE pordId IN(SELECT [PreOrderId] 
				FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] 
				WHERE ISNULL([PreOrderId],0) <> 0)

DELETE FROM @PordTbl 
WHERE pordId IN(SELECT [PreOrderId] 
				FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] 
				WHERE ISNULL([PreOrderId],0) <> 0)
/*---------------------------*/
/*------------ PreOrder ------------*/	
		
DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails_Lang]
WHERE [VerificationRequestDetailId] IN(SELECT [VerificationRequestDetailId]      
										FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails]
										WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
																		FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
																		WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 10
																		AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT pordId FROM @PordTbl)))

DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails]
WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
								FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
								WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 10
								AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT pordId FROM @PordTbl))

/**/
DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests_Lang]
WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
								FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
								WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 10
								AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT pordId FROM @PordTbl))

DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 10
AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT pordId FROM @PordTbl)

/**/

DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderPrdGroupMustExistReasons_Lang]
WHERE [PrdGroupMustExistReasonId] IN(SELECT [PrdGroupMustExistReasonId]		 
										FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderPrdGroupMustExistReasons]
										WHERE [PreOrderId] IN(SELECT pordId FROM @PordTbl))

DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderPrdGroupMustExistReasons]
WHERE [PreOrderId] IN(SELECT pordId FROM @PordTbl)
/**/
DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderCommissions_Lang]
WHERE [PreOrderCommissionId] IN(SELECT [PreOrderCommissionId]      
								FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderCommissions]
								WHERE [CommPreOrderId] IN(SELECT pordId FROM @PordTbl))

DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderCommissions]
WHERE [CommPreOrderId] IN(SELECT pordId FROM @PordTbl)
/**/
DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderPayments_Lang]
WHERE PreOrderPaymentId IN(SELECT [PreOrderPaymentId]		    
							FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderPayments]
							WHERE [pordpayPreOrderId] IN(SELECT pordId FROM @PordTbl))

DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderPayments]
WHERE [pordpayPreOrderId] IN(SELECT pordId FROM @PordTbl)
/**/
DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItemSpecification_Lang]
WHERE [PreOrderItemSpcflID] IN(SELECT [PreOrderItemSpcflID]		
								FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItemSpecification]
								WHERE [PreOrderItemId] IN (SELECT [PreOrderItemID]
															FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems]
															WHERE [PreOrderId] IN(SELECT pordId FROM @PordTbl)))
		
DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItemSpecification]
WHERE [PreOrderItemId] IN (SELECT [PreOrderItemID]
							FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems]
							WHERE [PreOrderId] IN(SELECT pordId FROM @PordTbl))
/**/
DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems_Lang]
WHERE PreOrderItemID IN (SELECT [PreOrderItemID]
							FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems]
							WHERE [PreOrderId] IN(SELECT pordId FROM @PordTbl))

DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems]
WHERE [PreOrderId] IN(SELECT pordId FROM @PordTbl)
/**/
DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder_Lang]
WHERE [PreOrderID] IN(SELECT pordId FROM @PordTbl)

DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
WHERE PreOrderID IN(SELECT pordId FROM @PordTbl)
/*---------------------------*/
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[WSspDeletePreOrderItemsAll]






/*NKR14001104*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[WSspDeletePreOrderItemsAll]
(
	/*
		EXEC [WSspDeletePreOrderItemsAll] 0
	*/
	@PreOrderId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
	/**/
	DECLARE @Result INT
	/**/
	BEGIN TRY
		/**/
		DELETE WSPreOrderItemSpecification_Lang 
		WHERE PreOrderItemSpcflID IN (
										SELECT POIS.PreOrderItemSpcflID 
										FROM WSPreOrderItemSpecification POIS 
										WHERE POIS.PreOrderItemId IN (
																		SELECT POI.PreOrderItemID 
																		FROM WSPreOrderItems POI 
																		WHERE POI.PreOrderId = @PreOrderId
																		)
									 )
		DELETE WSPreOrderItemSpecification WHERE PreOrderItemId IN (SELECT POI.PreOrderItemID FROM WSPreOrderItems POI WHERE POI.PreOrderId = @PreOrderId)
		/**/
		DELETE WSPreOrderItems_Lang WHERE PreOrderItemID IN (SELECT POI.PreOrderItemID FROM WSPreOrderItems POI WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderId)
		DELETE WSPreOrderItems WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderId
		/**/
		SET @Result = 1
		/**/
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		SET @Result = 0
	END CATCH
	/**/
	SELECT @Result AS [Result]
	/**/
END

-- [dbo].[WSspDeletePreOrderItemWithLinkedPrd]





/*RZ14000525*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspDeletePreOrderItemWithLinkedPrd]
(		
/*
	exec WSspDeletePreOrderItemWithLinkedPrd 2855,9699
*/
@PreOrderId INT,
@PreOrderItemId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 
		/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
		/*------------------------ Delete items by linked -----------------*/
		DECLARE @ProductId INT
		SET @ProductId = (SELECT TOP 1 [pordIPrdId] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] WHERE [PreOrderItemID] = @PreOrderItemId) 
		
		
		
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItemSpecification_Lang]  
				WHERE PreOrderItemSpcflID IN (SELECT PreOrderItemSpcflID 
											  FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItemSpecification] 
											  WHERE PreOrderItemId IN (SELECT I.PreOrderItemID 
																	   FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] I 
																	   WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderId
																	   AND [pordIPrdId] IN(SELECT 
																								(CASE WHEN PrdId = @ProductId 
																									THEN LinkedPrdId
																									ELSE PrdId									
																								END) [LinkedPrdId]			 
																							FROM [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts]	
																							WHERE ([PrdId] = @ProductId	
																							OR 	[LinkedPrdId] = @ProductId)			
																							)))

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItemSpecification] 
		WHERE PreOrderItemId IN (SELECT I.PreOrderItemID FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] I 
								WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderId
								AND [pordIPrdId] IN(SELECT 
															(CASE WHEN PrdId = @ProductId 
																THEN LinkedPrdId
																ELSE PrdId									
															END) [LinkedPrdId]			 
													FROM [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts]	
													WHERE ([PrdId] = @ProductId	
													OR 	[LinkedPrdId] = @ProductId)			
													))

		/*-----------------------*/

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems_Lang]  
		WHERE PreOrderItemID IN (SELECT I.PreOrderItemID FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] I 
								WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderId
								AND [pordIPrdId] IN(SELECT 
															(CASE WHEN PrdId = @ProductId 
																THEN LinkedPrdId
																ELSE PrdId									
															END) [LinkedPrdId]			 
													FROM [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts]	
													WHERE ([PrdId] = @ProductId	
													OR 	[LinkedPrdId] = @ProductId)			
													))

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] 
		WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderId
		AND [pordIPrdId] IN (SELECT 
								 (CASE WHEN PrdId = @ProductId 
									   THEN LinkedPrdId
									   ELSE PrdId									
								 END) [LinkedPrdId]			 
							FROM [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts]	
							WHERE ([PrdId] = @ProductId	
							OR 	[LinkedPrdId] = @ProductId)			
							)
		
		/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
		/*---------------------- Delete main item -----------------------*/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItemSpecification_Lang]  
			WHERE PreOrderItemSpcflID IN (SELECT PreOrderItemSpcflID 
											FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItemSpecification] 
											WHERE PreOrderItemId = @PreOrderItemId)
						
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItemSpecification] WHERE PreOrderItemId = @PreOrderItemId

		/*-----------------------*/

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems_Lang]  WHERE PreOrderItemID = @PreOrderItemId
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems]  WHERE PreOrderItemID = @PreOrderItemId

		/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
		SELECT 1 AS Result
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	SELECT 0 AS Result
END CATCH
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[WSspDeleteProductAndPriceWithDependencies]






/*RZ14021123*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspDeleteProductAndPriceWithDependencies]
(		
/*
	exec WSspDeleteProductAndPriceWithDependencies 15
*/
@ProductId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
/*------------------------- Find PreOrder,Order,BuyOrder,Doc ------------------*/
DECLARE @CanDelete BIT = 1, @Result NVARCHAR(50) = N''

IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] WHERE [PreOrderID] IN(SELECT I.PreOrderId 
																	FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] I
																	WHERE I.pordIPrdId = @ProductId)))/*Pord*/
BEGIN
	SET @CanDelete = 0
	IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
		SET @Result = '-1'
	ELSE 
		SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-1'
END
/**/
IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] WHERE [OrderID] IN(SELECT I.OrderId 
																	FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] I
																	WHERE I.ordIPrdId = @ProductId)))/*Ord*/
BEGIN
	SET @CanDelete = 0
	IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
		SET @Result = '-2'
	ELSE 
		SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-2'
END
/**/
IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrder] WHERE [BuyOrderID] IN(SELECT I.BuyOrderId 
																	FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] I
																	WHERE I.bordIPrdId = @ProductId)))/*Bord*/
BEGIN
	SET @CanDelete = 0
	IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
		SET @Result = '-3'
	ELSE 
		SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-3'
END
/**/
IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] WHERE [DocID] IN(SELECT I.DocId 
																	FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems] I
																	WHERE I.PrdId = @ProductId)))/*Doc*/
BEGIN
	SET @CanDelete = 0
	IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
		SET @Result = '-4'
	ELSE 
		SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-4'
END	
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------*/								
IF(@CanDelete = 1)
BEGIN
/**/
	BEGIN TRY
	/**/
		BEGIN TRANSACTION 
			/*------------------------------------------------*/
			/*------------ FilesDirectory ------------*/	
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory_Lang]
			WHERE [FileDirectoryID] IN(SELECT [FileDirectoryID]     
										FROM [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory]
										WHERE [FileDirectoryTypeId] = -60
										AND [TypeOprObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
																	FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
																	WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
																	AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
																					FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
																					WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
																					OR [FOSysPartId] = 70)))

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory]
			WHERE [FileDirectoryTypeId] = -60
			AND [TypeOprObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
										FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
										WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
										AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
														FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
														WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
														OR [FOSysPartId] = 70))
			/*------------ DyForm ------------*/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails_Lang]
			WHERE [VerificationRequestDetailId] IN(SELECT [VerificationRequestDetailId]      
													FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails]
													WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
																					FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
																					WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 80
																					AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
																												FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
																												WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
																												AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
																																FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
																																WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
																																OR [FOSysPartId] = 70))))

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails]
			WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
											FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
											WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 80
											AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
																		FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
																		WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
																		AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
																						FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
																						WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
																						OR [FOSysPartId] = 70)))

			/**/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests_Lang]
			WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
											FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
											WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 80
											AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
																		FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
																		WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
																		AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
																						FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
																						WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
																						OR [FOSysPartId] = 70)))

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
			WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 80
			AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
										FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
										WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
										AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
														FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
														WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
														OR [FOSysPartId] = 70))

			/**/
			DELETE FROM dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues_Lang]  
			WHERE FieldValueId IN (SELECT FormFieldValueId 
									FROM dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues] 
									WHERE MasterValueId IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
															FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues] 
															WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
															AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
																			FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
																			WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
																			OR [FOSysPartId] = 70)))
		
			DELETE FROM dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues] 
			WHERE MasterValueId IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
									FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues] 
									WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
									AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
													FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
													WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
													OR [FOSysPartId] = 70))

			/**/
			DELETE FROM dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValueDetails] 
			WHERE MasterValueId IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
									FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
									WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
									AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
													FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
													WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
													OR [FOSysPartId] = 70))
			/**/		
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
			WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
			AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
							FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
							WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
							OR [FOSysPartId] = 70)
								
			/*------------ ProductsRate ------------*/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductsRate]
			WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId

			/*------------ ProductsUserDiscount ------------*/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductsUserDiscount_Lang]
			WHERE [ProductsUserDiscountId] IN(SELECT [ProductsUserDiscountId] 
												FROM [dbo].[WSProductsUserDiscount]
												WHERE [DiscountProductId] = @ProductId)

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductsUserDiscount]
			WHERE [DiscountProductId] = @ProductId

			/*------------ ProductRelationLimit ------------*/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductRelationLimit]
			WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId

			/*------------ LinkedProducts ------------*/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts]
			WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId
			OR [LinkedPrdId] = @ProductId

			/*------------ UserFavproductItems ------------*/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSUserFavproductItems_Lang]
			WHERE [UserFavproductItemId] IN(SELECT [UserFavproductItemId] 
												FROM [dbo].[WSUserFavproductItems]
												WHERE [FavprdIPrdId] = @ProductId)

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSUserFavproductItems]
			WHERE [FavprdIPrdId] = @ProductId
				
			/*------------ DiscountCode ------------*/	
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSDiscountCodeHistory]
			WHERE [DiscountCodeId] IN(SELECT [DiscountCodeId]      
										FROM [dbo].[WSDiscountCode]
										WHERE [DisPrdId] = @ProductId)
		
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSDiscountCodeHistory]
			WHERE [DisPrdId] = @ProductId
			/**/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSDiscountCode_Lang]
			WHERE [DiscountCodeId] IN(SELECT [DiscountCodeId]      
										FROM [dbo].[WSDiscountCode]
										WHERE [DisPrdId] = @ProductId)

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSDiscountCode]
			WHERE [DisPrdId] = @ProductId
				
			/*------------ CmsHomePageSectionItems ------------*/	
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmCmsHomePageSectionItems]
			WHERE [SectionItemPrdId] = @ProductId
				
			/*------------ ProductSpecification ------------*/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductSpecification_Lang]
			WHERE [ProductSpecificationID] IN(SELECT [ProductSpecificationID] 
												FROM [dbo].[WSProductSpecification]
												WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId)

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductSpecification]
			WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId
				
			/*------------ ProductGallery ------------*/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductGallery]
			WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId


			/*------------ ProductPicture ------------*/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductPicture_Lang]
			WHERE [PrdPictureID] IN(SELECT [PrdPictureID] 
									FROM [dbo].[WSProductPicture]
									WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId)

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductPicture]
			WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId

			/*------------ ProductCost ------------*/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductCost_Lang]
			WHERE [ProductCostID] IN(SELECT [ProductCostID] 
									FROM [dbo].[WSProductCost]
									WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId)

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductCost]
			WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId

			/*------------ ProductPrice ------------*/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductPrice_Lang]
			WHERE [PrdPriceID] IN(SELECT [PrdPriceID] 
									FROM [dbo].[WSProductPrice]
									WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId)

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductPrice]
			WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId				
			/*------------ CrmProjectProductHistory ------------*/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectProductHistory_Lang]
			WHERE [ProjectProductHistoryId] IN(SELECT [ProjectProductHistoryId]     
									FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectProductHistory]
									WHERE [PrjPrdHProductId] = @ProductId)

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectProductHistory]
			WHERE [PrjPrdHProductId] = @ProductId
			/*------------ Produc ------------*/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProducts_Lang]
			WHERE [ProductID] = @ProductId

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
			WHERE [ProductID] = @ProductId
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			SET @Result = '1'
		COMMIT
	/**/
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
	/**/
		IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
			ROLLBACK
		SET @Result = '0'
	/**/
	END CATCH
/**/
END
/*----------------------------------------------------------------*/
SELECT @Result AS [Result]
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*========================================================*/
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[WSspDeleteProductRelationLimitAllByType]







/*RZ991007*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspDeleteProductRelationLimitAllByType]
(
/*   
	exec WSspDeleteProductRelationLimitAllByType 1,1,15 
*/
@ProductRelationTypeId INT,
@ObjectId INT,
@PrdGroupId INT
)		
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*======================= Select Objects ========================*/
DECLARE @ObjTmpTable TABLE (
	[ObjectId] INT
)
/*=============== Set objects(children) =========================*/
IF(@ProductRelationTypeId = 8)/*ProjectCategory*/
BEGIN
/**/
	;WITH cte AS 
	(	  
		SELECT 
			PC.[ProjectCategoryId]				
		FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectCategory] PC
		WHERE PC.[ParentId] = @ObjectId
		UNION ALL
		SELECT 
			PCI.[ProjectCategoryId]				
		FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectCategory] PCI
		INNER JOIN cte C
		ON C.[ProjectCategoryId] = PCI.[ParentId]	
	)
	
	INSERT INTO @ObjTmpTable 
		([ObjectId])
	SELECT @ObjectId
	UNION ALL
	SELECT DISTINCT * FROM cte; 
/**/
END
/*========================== DELETE =============================*/
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION
		/*---------------------------------*/
		IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 [ObjectId] FROM @ObjTmpTable),0) > 0) /*for objects that have multi level for example prjCategory*/
		BEGIN
		/**/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductRelationLimit]
			WHERE [PrdId] IN (SELECT [ProductID]
							   FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] P			
							   WHERE P.[PrdGroupId] = @PrdGroupId)
			AND [ObjectId] IN (SELECT TT.[ObjectId] FROM @ObjTmpTable TT)
			AND [ProductRelationTypeId] = @ProductRelationTypeId				
		/**/
		END
		ELSE IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 [ObjectId] FROM @ObjTmpTable),0) = 0) /*for just an object*/
		BEGIN
		/**/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductRelationLimit]
			WHERE [PrdId] IN (SELECT [ProductID]
							   FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] P			
							   WHERE P.[PrdGroupId] = @PrdGroupId)
			AND [ObjectId] = @ObjectId
			AND [ProductRelationTypeId] = @ProductRelationTypeId
		/**/
		END
		/*---------------------------------*/
		SELECT 1 AS Result
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	SELECT 0 AS Result
END CATCH
/**/
END

GO
-- [dbo].[WSspDeleteProductRelationLimitByPrdIdsAndType]










/*RZ991007*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspDeleteProductRelationLimitByPrdIdsAndType]
(
/*   
	exec WSspDeleteProductRelationLimitByPrdIdsAndType 1,1,'1,3,5' 
*/
@ProductRelationTypeId INT,
@ObjectId INT,
@PrdIds NVARCHAR(MAX)/*Comma separator*/
)		
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*======================= Create prdTmpTbable ===================*/
DECLARE @PrdTmpTable TABLE (
	[PrdId] INT
)
INSERT INTO @PrdTmpTable ([PrdId])
SELECT CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,'0') AS INT) 
FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString](@PrdIds,',')
WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,'0') AS INT)  > 0
/*======================= Select Objects ========================*/
DECLARE @ObjTmpTable TABLE (
	[ObjectId] INT
)
/*=============== Set objects(children) =========================*/
IF(@ProductRelationTypeId = 8)/*ProjectCategory*/
BEGIN
/**/
	;WITH cte AS 
	(	  
		SELECT 
			PC.[ProjectCategoryId]				
		FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectCategory] PC
		WHERE PC.[ParentId] = @ObjectId
		UNION ALL
		SELECT 
			PCI.[ProjectCategoryId]				
		FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectCategory] PCI
		INNER JOIN cte C
		ON C.[ProjectCategoryId] = PCI.[ParentId]	
	)
	
	INSERT INTO @ObjTmpTable 
		([ObjectId])
	SELECT @ObjectId
	UNION ALL
	SELECT DISTINCT * FROM cte; 
/**/
END
/*========================== DELETE =============================*/
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION
		/*---------------------------------*/
		IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 [ObjectId] FROM @ObjTmpTable),0) > 0) /*for objects that have multi level for example prjCategory*/
		BEGIN
		/**/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductRelationLimit]
			WHERE [PrdId] IN (SELECT PTT.[PrdId] FROM @PrdTmpTable PTT)
			AND [ObjectId] IN (SELECT TT.[ObjectId] FROM @ObjTmpTable TT)
			AND [ProductRelationTypeId] = @ProductRelationTypeId				
		/**/
		END
		ELSE IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 [ObjectId] FROM @ObjTmpTable),0) = 0) /*for just an object*/
		BEGIN
		/**/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductRelationLimit]
			WHERE [PrdId] IN (SELECT PTT.[PrdId] FROM @PrdTmpTable PTT)
			AND [ObjectId] = @ObjectId
			AND [ProductRelationTypeId] = @ProductRelationTypeId
		/**/
		END
		/*---------------------------------*/
		SELECT 1 AS Result
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	SELECT 0 AS Result
END CATCH
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[WSspDeleteProductWithDependencies]






/*RZ14021123*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspDeleteProductWithDependencies]
(		
/*
	exec WSspDeleteProductWithDependencies 15
*/
@ProductId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
/*------------------------- Find PreOrder,Order,BuyOrder,Doc ------------------*/
DECLARE @CanDelete BIT = 1, @Result NVARCHAR(50) = N''

IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] WHERE [PreOrderID] IN(SELECT I.PreOrderId 
																	FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] I
																	WHERE I.pordIPrdId = @ProductId)))/*Pord*/
BEGIN
	SET @CanDelete = 0
	IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
		SET @Result = '-1'
	ELSE 
		SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-1'
END
/**/
IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] WHERE [OrderID] IN(SELECT I.OrderId 
																	FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] I
																	WHERE I.ordIPrdId = @ProductId)))/*Ord*/
BEGIN
	SET @CanDelete = 0
	IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
		SET @Result = '-2'
	ELSE 
		SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-2'
END
/**/
IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrder] WHERE [BuyOrderID] IN(SELECT I.BuyOrderId 
																	FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] I
																	WHERE I.bordIPrdId = @ProductId)))/*Bord*/
BEGIN
	SET @CanDelete = 0
	IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
		SET @Result = '-3'
	ELSE 
		SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-3'
END
/**/
IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] WHERE [DocID] IN(SELECT I.DocId 
																	FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems] I
																	WHERE I.PrdId = @ProductId)))/*Doc*/
BEGIN
	SET @CanDelete = 0
	IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
		SET @Result = '-4'
	ELSE 
		SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-4'
END
/**/
IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSProductCost] WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId))/*PrdCost*/
BEGIN
	SET @CanDelete = 0
	IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
		SET @Result = '-5'
	ELSE 
		SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-5'
END
/**/
IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSProductPrice] WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId))/*PrdPrice*/
BEGIN
	SET @CanDelete = 0
	IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
		SET @Result = '-6'
	ELSE 
		SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-6'
END
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------*/								
IF(@CanDelete = 1)
BEGIN
/**/
	BEGIN TRY
	/**/
		BEGIN TRANSACTION 
			/*------------------------------------------------*/
			/*------------ FilesDirectory ------------*/	
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory_Lang]
			WHERE [FileDirectoryID] IN(SELECT [FileDirectoryID]     
										FROM [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory]
										WHERE [FileDirectoryTypeId] = -60
										AND [TypeOprObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
																	FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
																	WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
																	AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
																					FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
																					WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
																					OR [FOSysPartId] = 70)))

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory]
			WHERE [FileDirectoryTypeId] = -60
			AND [TypeOprObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
										FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
										WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
										AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
														FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
														WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
														OR [FOSysPartId] = 70))
			/*------------ DyForm ------------*/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails_Lang]
			WHERE [VerificationRequestDetailId] IN(SELECT [VerificationRequestDetailId]      
													FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails]
													WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
																					FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
																					WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 80
																					AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
																												FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
																												WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
																												AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
																																FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
																																WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
																																OR [FOSysPartId] = 70))))

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails]
			WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
											FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
											WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 80
											AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
																		FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
																		WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
																		AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
																						FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
																						WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
																						OR [FOSysPartId] = 70)))

			/**/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests_Lang]
			WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
											FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
											WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 80
											AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
																		FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
																		WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
																		AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
																						FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
																						WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
																						OR [FOSysPartId] = 70)))

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
			WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 80
			AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
										FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
										WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
										AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
														FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
														WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
														OR [FOSysPartId] = 70))

			/**/
			DELETE FROM dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues_Lang]  
			WHERE FieldValueId IN (SELECT FormFieldValueId 
									FROM dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues] 
									WHERE MasterValueId IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
															FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues] 
															WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
															AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
																			FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
																			WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
																			OR [FOSysPartId] = 70)))
		
			DELETE FROM dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues] 
			WHERE MasterValueId IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
									FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues] 
									WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
									AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
													FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
													WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
													OR [FOSysPartId] = 70))

			/**/
			DELETE FROM dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValueDetails] 
			WHERE MasterValueId IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
									FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
									WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
									AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
													FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
													WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
													OR [FOSysPartId] = 70))
			/**/		
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
			WHERE [MasterObjectId] = @ProductId
			AND [FormId] IN(SELECT [FormId]      
							FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
							WHERE [CrmSysPartId] = 70 
							OR [FOSysPartId] = 70)
								
			/*------------ ProductsRate ------------*/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductsRate]
			WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId

			/*------------ ProductsUserDiscount ------------*/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductsUserDiscount_Lang]
			WHERE [ProductsUserDiscountId] IN(SELECT [ProductsUserDiscountId] 
												FROM [dbo].[WSProductsUserDiscount]
												WHERE [DiscountProductId] = @ProductId)

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductsUserDiscount]
			WHERE [DiscountProductId] = @ProductId

			/*------------ ProductRelationLimit ------------*/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductRelationLimit]
			WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId

			/*------------ LinkedProducts ------------*/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts]
			WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId
			OR [LinkedPrdId] = @ProductId

			/*------------ UserFavproductItems ------------*/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSUserFavproductItems_Lang]
			WHERE [UserFavproductItemId] IN(SELECT [UserFavproductItemId] 
												FROM [dbo].[WSUserFavproductItems]
												WHERE [FavprdIPrdId] = @ProductId)

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSUserFavproductItems]
			WHERE [FavprdIPrdId] = @ProductId
				
			/*------------ DiscountCode ------------*/	
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSDiscountCodeHistory]
			WHERE [DiscountCodeId] IN(SELECT [DiscountCodeId]      
										FROM [dbo].[WSDiscountCode]
										WHERE [DisPrdId] = @ProductId)
		
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSDiscountCodeHistory]
			WHERE [DisPrdId] = @ProductId
			/**/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSDiscountCode_Lang]
			WHERE [DiscountCodeId] IN(SELECT [DiscountCodeId]      
										FROM [dbo].[WSDiscountCode]
										WHERE [DisPrdId] = @ProductId)

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSDiscountCode]
			WHERE [DisPrdId] = @ProductId
				
			/*------------ CmsHomePageSectionItems ------------*/	
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmCmsHomePageSectionItems]
			WHERE [SectionItemPrdId] = @ProductId
				
			/*------------ ProductSpecification ------------*/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductSpecification_Lang]
			WHERE [ProductSpecificationID] IN(SELECT [ProductSpecificationID] 
												FROM [dbo].[WSProductSpecification]
												WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId)

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductSpecification]
			WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId
				
			/*------------ ProductGallery ------------*/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductGallery]
			WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId


			/*------------ ProductPicture ------------*/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductPicture_Lang]
			WHERE [PrdPictureID] IN(SELECT [PrdPictureID] 
									FROM [dbo].[WSProductPicture]
									WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId)

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProductPicture]
			WHERE [PrdId] = @ProductId
			/*------------ CrmProjectProductHistory ------------*/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectProductHistory_Lang]
			WHERE [ProjectProductHistoryId] IN(SELECT [ProjectProductHistoryId]     
												FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectProductHistory]
												WHERE [PrjPrdHProductId] = @ProductId)

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectProductHistory]
			WHERE [PrjPrdHProductId] = @ProductId
			/*------------ Produc ------------*/
			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProducts_Lang]
			WHERE [ProductID] = @ProductId

			DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
			WHERE [ProductID] = @ProductId
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			SET @Result = '1'
		COMMIT
	/**/
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
	/**/
		IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
			ROLLBACK
		SET @Result = '0'
	/**/
	END CATCH
/**/
END
/*----------------------------------------------------------------*/	
SELECT @Result AS [Result]
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*========================================================*/
/**/
END









GO
